# 病理学
## 细胞和组织的损伤
不属于细胞适应性改变的是
A. atrophy
B. pypertrophy
C. hyperplasia
D. metaplasia
E. degeneration
【参考答案】 E

属于不可复性损伤的是
A. fatty degeneration
B. cell edema
C. mucoid degeneration
D. amyloidosis
E. necrosis
【参考答案】 E

结节性动脉炎血管壁出现的红染、纤维素样物质,称为
A. apoptosis
B. necrosis
C. gangrene
D. caseous necrosis
E. fibrinous necrosis
【参考答案】 E

心脏褐色萎缩,心肌细胞內的黄褐色物质是
A. lipofuscin
B. melanin
C. hemosiderin
D. calcification
E. bilirubin
【参考答案】 A

黏膜坏死脱落而遗留的深达黏膜下层的缺损,称为
A. erosion
B. fistula
C. sinus
D. cavity
E. ulcer
【参考答案】 E

表皮细胞属于
A. stable cell
B. labile cell
C. permanent cell
D. stroma cell
E. stem cell
【参考答案】 B

细胞内、血管壁和结缔组织内出现均质、红染、玻璃样物质,称为
A. fatty degeneration
B. hyaline degeneration
C. amyloid degeneration
D. mucoid degeneration
E. hydropic degeneration
【参考答案】 B

一种分化成熟组织取代另外一种分化成熟组织,称为
A. atrophy
B. hypertrophy
C. hyperplasia
D. metaplasia
E. degeneration
【参考答案】 D

不属于萎缩的是
A. 80岁老人的脑
B. 晚期癌症患者的心脏
C. 空洞型肾结核的肾实质
D. 小儿麻痹症的下肢肌肉
E. 恶病质患者的脂肪组织
【参考答案】 C

癌症患者首先发生萎缩的组织是
A. 脂肪组织
B. 骨骼肌
C. 心肌
D. 肝细胞
E. 神经元
【参考答案】 A

符合萎缩的描述是
A. 器官体积小于正常即为萎缩
B. 萎缩的器官间质增生
C. 萎缩细胞功能正常
D. 萎缩器官的细胞数目正常
E. 供血断绝引起萎缩
【参考答案】 B

符合萎缩的病变是
A. 淋巴结结核
B. Di George 综合征的胸腺
C. 老年妇女的子宫内膜
D. 先天性食管闭锁
E. 主动脉夹层
【参考答案】 C

不属于萎缩的是
A. 高血压的左心室
B. 80岁老年人的脑
C. 慢性高血压的肾实质
D. 脑积水的脑实质
E. 晚期癌症患者的心脏
【参考答案】 A

食管癌患者体内最先萎缩的是
A. 心肌细胞
B. 骨骼肌细胞
C. 肝细胞
D. 神经细胞
E. 脂肪细胞
【参考答案】 E

属于生理性萎缩的是
A. 垂体坏死引起的乳腺退缩
B. 青年男性睾丸生精细胞大量减少
C. 小儿麻痹引起的下肢萎缩
D. 青春期胸腺萎缩
E. 动脉粥样硬化症引起的脑萎缩
【参考答案】 D

不属于萎缩的器官是
A. 高血压病的肾脏
B. 肾切除术后,保留的对侧肾脏
C. 慢性肾小球肾炎的肾脏
D. 肾盂积水的肾脏
E. 动脉粥样硬化症的肾脏
【参考答案】 B

与肝脂肪变性无关的是
A. 酗酒
B. 四氯化碳中毒
C. 摄入过多的脂肪
D. 糖尿病
E. 白喉杆菌感染
【参考答案】 C

不符合脂肪变性的描述是
A. 槟榔肝时,肝小叶周边区脂肪变性最明显
B. 磷中毒时,肝小叶中央区脂肪变性最明显
C. 砷中毒时,可引起心肌脂肪变性
D. 白喉时,可引起“虎斑心”
E. 严重的贫血时,肾近曲管上皮可出现脂肪变性
【参考答案】 B

不符合脂肪变性的是
A. 肝细胞内出现大小不等的脂滴
B. 巨噬细胞内出现大小不等的脂滴
C. 心肌细胞内出现大小不等的脂滴
D. 脂肪肉瘤细胞内出现大小不等的脂滴
E. 近曲小管上皮细胞内出现大小不等的脂滴
【参考答案】 D

不引起肝细胞脂肪变性的是
A. 酒精中毒
B. 四氯化碳中毒
C. 糖尿病
D. 白喉
E. 高脂肪饮食
【参考答案】 E

关于肝脂肪变性的描述,正确的是
A. 肝淤血时,脂肪变性首先发生在小叶周围
B. 磷中毒时,脂肪变性主要发生在小叶中央区
C. 轻度肝脂肪变性时,肉眼下肝可无明显改变
D. 重度肝脂肪变性时,肝脏缩小
E. 脂肪变性的肝细胞的细胞浆疏松,呈空网状
【参考答案】 C

关于心肌脂肪变性的描述,错误的是
A. 白喉外毒素可引起心肌脂肪变性
B. 磷、砷、氯仿可引起心肌脂肪变性
C. 严重贫血可引起心肌脂肪变性
D. 心肌脂肪变性严重影响心脏功能
E. 心肌内出现串珠状、细小脂肪空泡
【参考答案】 D

关于肾脂肪变性的描述,错误的是
A. 与肾小球毛细血管通透性升高有关
B. 脂肪变性主要累及远曲管上皮细胞
C. 可见于中毒
D. 可见于脂性肾病
E. 可见于严重贫血
【参考答案】 B

脂肪变性时,脂滴位于
A. 高尔基器
B. 线粒体
C. 溶酶体
D. 内质网
E. 胞浆基质
【参考答案】 D

纤维素样坏死不见于
A. 风湿病
B. 恶性高血压
C. 动脉粥肿
D. 系统性红斑狼疮
E. 类风湿性关节炎
【参考答案】 C

纤维素样坏死的病变内不出现
A. 粘多糖
B. 免疫球蛋白
C. 纤维蛋白
D. 基底膜样物质
E. 胶原崩解产物
【参考答案】 D

结节性多动脉炎动脉壁的红染颗粒状物质是
A. 水变性
B. 脂肪变性
C. 玻璃样变性
D. 淀粉样变性
E. 纤维素样坏死
【参考答案】 E

纤维组织内颗粒状、团块状、强嗜酸红染的无结构物质,状似纤维素,此病变称为
A. 水变性
B. 脂肪变性
C. 玻璃样变性
D. 纤维素样坏死
E. 钙化
【参考答案】 D

纤维素样坏死可见于
A. 风湿病
B. 良性高血压
C. 动脉粥肿
D. 瘢痕
E. 甲状腺功能低下
【参考答案】 A

纤维素样坏死可见于
A. 良性高血压
B. 恶性高血压
C. 动脉粥肿
D. 瘢痕
E. 甲状腺功能低下
【参考答案】 B

纤维素样坏死可见于
A. 良性高血压
B. 甲状腺功能低下
C. 动脉粥肿
D. 系统性红斑狼疮
E. 瘢痕
【参考答案】 D

纤维素样坏死可见于
A. 良性高血压
B. 瘢痕
C. 动脉粥肿
D. 甲状腺功能低下
E. 类风湿性关节炎
【参考答案】 E

玻璃样变性不发生于
A. 结缔组织
B. 血管壁
C. 浆细胞
D. 肝细胞
E. 肾远曲小管上皮细胞
【参考答案】 E

结缔组织玻璃样变的错误描述是
A. 常见于瘢痕组织
B. 常见于纤维化肾小球
C. 纤维母细胞增多
D. 胶原纤维融合为梁状、带状或片状
E. 常见于粥瘤
【参考答案】 C

血管壁玻璃样变多见于
A. 大动脉
B. 中型动脉
C. 细动脉
D. 大静脉
E. 小静脉
【参考答案】 C

关于血管壁玻璃样变,错误的描述是
A. 常见于高血压病
B. 常见于肾、脑、脾、视网膜细动脉
C. 血管壁变硬、管腔狭窄
D. 可引起组织缺血
E. 玻璃样物质由免疫球蛋白和基底膜样物构成
【参考答案】 E

关于细胞内玻璃样变错误的描述是
A. 肾近曲小管上皮内的玻璃样小滴由重吸收的血浆蛋白构成
B. 近曲管上皮玻璃样变多见于肾盂肾炎
C. 肝细胞内的 Mallory 小体由前角蛋白构成
D. Mallory 小体多见于酒精中毒时
E. 浆细胞内的玻璃样小体由免疫球蛋白构成
【参考答案】 B

女,40岁,患者因血尿、蛋白尿、管型尿,经治疗无明显好转。四年后,因尿毒症死亡。尸体解剖发现两肾对称性萎缩,表面呈细颗粒状。组织学检查,大量肾小球纤维化,并呈现均匀红染,半透明小团。这些肾小球的病变属于
A. 纤维素样坏死
B. 淀粉样变性
C. 玻璃样变性
D. 脂肪变性
E. 水变性
【参考答案】 C

男,30岁,酗酒时间约8年余,日消费北京二锅头酒约500ml。近半年来经常出现恶心、呕吐、食欲不振。肝穿刺显示肝细胞广泛脂肪变性,肝细胞灶状坏死。肝细胞内可见大小不等的红染的半透明小体。这些透明小体为
A. 前角蛋白微丝
B. 免疫球蛋白
C. 血浆蛋白
D. 乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原
E. 肝糖原
【参考答案】 A

男,55岁,高血压病史已20余年。手术切除脾脏,切片可见脾中央小动脉管壁增厚,管腔狭窄,管壁内可见均匀红染、半透明物质。这些半透明物质为
A. 淀粉样物质
B. 血浆蛋白和基底膜样物质
C. 胶原纤维
D. 免疫球蛋白
E. 脂蛋白
【参考答案】 B

不符合淀粉样物质的描述是
A. 组织化学反应与淀粉相似
B. 丙种球蛋白沉积而成
C. 电镜下呈纤细丝状
D. 镜下呈淡红色均质状
E. 结合粘多糖的蛋白质
【参考答案】 B

不符合淀粉样物质的描述是
A. 可见于骨髓瘤
B. 可见于结核病
C. 可见于慢性化脓性炎
D. 可见于慢性炎症
E. 可见于结节性多动脉炎
【参考答案】 E

不符合变性的病变是
A. 间质内类粘液的积聚
B. 肝细胞内出现脂滴
C. 血管壁出现半透明、均匀红染的物质
D. 心肌细胞内水分增多
E. 细胞核破碎
【参考答案】 E

粘液样变性常见于
A. 甲状腺功能低下的皮下组织
B. 高血压的心脏
C. 硬癌的间质
D. 颗粒性固缩肾
E. 瘢痕
【参考答案】 A

慢性乙型肝炎时,电镜下毛玻璃细胞胞浆内可见
A. 扩张的光面内质网
B. Russell 小体
C. Mallory 小体
D. 脂质小体
E. 残存小体
【参考答案】 A

细胞内线粒体肿大、内质网扩张常见于
A. 水变性
B. 脂肪变性
C. 玻璃样变性
D. 纤维素样变性
E. 钙化
【参考答案】 A

肝细胞气球样变属于
A. 水变性
B. 脂肪变性
C. 玻璃样变性
D. 淀粉样变性
E. 纤维素样变性
【参考答案】 A

符合变性的描述是
A. 属不可复性损伤
B. 细胞功能丧失
C. 表现为异常物质的出现或正常物质显著增多
D. 细胞体积缩小, 伴功能下降
E. 细胞体积增大, 伴功能增强
【参考答案】 C

干酪样坏死灶周边部钙盐沉积称为
A. 水变性
B. 脂肪变性
C. 玻璃样变性
D. 纤维素样变性
E. 钙化
【参考答案】 E

形成脂褐素的细胞器是
A. 高尔基器
B. 粗面内质网
C. 光面内质网
D. 溶酶体
E. 线粒体
【参考答案】 D

不符合含铁血黄素的描述是
A. 常见于心肌细胞核两侧
B. 光镜下, 呈棕黄色, 具有折光性
C. 血红蛋白转化而来
D. 普鲁士蓝反应阳性
E. 常见于出血灶周围
【参考答案】 A

男, 60岁, 因胃癌广泛转移而死亡。尸体解剖检查, 心脏重200g。切片检查心肌细胞核两侧可见黄褐色细颗粒状色素, 此色素可能是
A. 黑色素
B. 胆色素
C. 含铁血黄素
D. 脂褐素
E. 前角蛋白聚集
【参考答案】 D

脂褐素电镜下为
A. 扩张的光面内质网
B. Russell 小体
C. Mallory 小体
D. 脂质小体
E. 残存小体
【参考答案】 E

干酪样坏死灶周围钙盐沉积, 称为
A. 溶解吸收
B. 分离排出
C. 机化
D. 钙化
E. 包裹
【参考答案】 D

硬脑膜出现了骨组织属于
A. 肥大
B. 增生
C. 再生
D. 化生
E. 机化
【参考答案】 D

甲状腺瘤间质钙化灶中有骨组织属于
A. 肥大
B. 增生
C. 再生
D. 化生
E. 机化
【参考答案】 D

肠扭转引起的坏死, 称为
A. 凝固性坏死
B. 液化性坏死
C. 气性坏疽
D. 干性坏疽
E. 湿性坏疽
【参考答案】 E

深部开放损伤合并厌气菌感染, 称为
A. 凝固性坏死
B. 液化性坏死
C. 气性坏疽
D. 干性坏疽
E. 湿性坏疽
【参考答案】 C

产后子宫感染引起的坏死属于
A. 凝固性坏死
B. 干酪样坏死
C. 液化性坏死
D. 湿性坏疽
E. 脂肪坏死
【参考答案】 D

气性坏疽是指
A. 结核杆菌感染
B. 坏死组织伴腐败菌感染
C. 葡萄球菌感染
D. 深部组织的产气荚膜杆菌、恶性水肿杆菌及腐败弧菌等厌气菌感染
E. 溶血性链球菌感染
【参考答案】 D

后果严重需紧急处理的坏死是
A. 乳房脂肪坏死
B. 下肢干性坏疽
C. 臀部气性坏疽
D. 肾局灶性梗死
E. 脾梗死
【参考答案】 C

不符合湿性坏疽的描述是
A. 合并腐败菌感染
B. 具有恶臭味
C. 常有全身中毒症状
D. 坏疽组织呈污秽黑褐色
E. 坏死组织与健康组织之间界限清楚
【参考答案】 E

男性, 23岁, 24小时前因车祸引起左下肢胫骨及腓骨开放性、粉碎性骨折, 入院清除固定后12小时, 出现左小腿及左足青紫、肿胀, 患者出现发热和全身不适。次日肿胀加剧, 累及左大腿, 伤口流出伴有气泡的的恶臭脓汁, 肿胀区按之有捻发音。左小腿的坏死为
A. 干性坏疽
B. 湿性坏疽
C. 气性坏疽
D. 液化性坏死
E. 干酪样坏死
【参考答案】 C

湿性坏疽常发生在
A. 脑、脾、肝
B. 脑、肠、子宫
C. 肺、肠、肝
D. 肺、肠、子宫
E. 肺、肾、脑
【参考答案】 D

不符合干性坏疽的描述是
A. 合并腐败菌感染
B. 常伴有全身中毒症状
C. 坏死组织呈污秽黑褐色
D. 坏死组织与健康组织之间界限清楚
E. 坏死部位动脉受阻, 而静脉回流通畅
【参考答案】 B

男, 68岁, 糖尿病史10年。近一年来左下肢行走后疼痛, 休息后好转。近两个月左拇趾变黑, 皱缩, 知觉消失。此种坏死是
A. 固缩坏死
B. 干性坏疽
C. 湿性坏疽
D. 气性坏疽
E. 液化性坏死
【参考答案】 B

下列哪个脏器不发生坏疽?
A. 肺
B. 下肢
C. 阑尾
D. 小肠
E. 脑
【参考答案】 E

血栓闭塞性脉管炎累及左下肢, 引起的坏死, 称为
A. 凝固性坏死
B. 液化性坏死
C. 气性坏疽
D. 干性坏疽
E. 湿性坏疽
【参考答案】 D

属于干性坏疽的是
A. 肠扭转引起的肠坏疽
B. 化脓性感染引起的肺坏疽
C. 坏疽性阑尾炎

不属于液化性坏死的是
A. 脾梗死
B. 脑梗死
C. 坏死性胰腺炎
D. 脂肪坏死
E. 脓肿
【参考答案】 A

不符合干酪样坏死的描述是
A. 呈淡黄色,均匀细腻
B. 状似纤维蛋白
C. 可发生钙化
D. 可发生液化
E. 常由结核杆菌引起
【参考答案】 B

液化性坏死常见于
A. 脑和胰腺
B. 肝
C. 脾
D. 肾
E. 小肠
【参考答案】 A

女,24岁,腋窝淋巴结肿大约半年,手术切除。手术标本为直径约2cm 的卵圆形淋巴结,切面大部为淡黄色、质软、易碎的坏死灶。镜下检查,坏死灶为红染的、颗粒状、无结构的物质,坏死灶周围可见类上皮细胞和多核巨细胞。以上所描述的坏死是
A. 梗死
B. 干性坏死
C. 气性坏死
D. 干酪样坏死
E. 液化性坏死
【参考答案】 D

B. 液化性坏死

D. 干性坏疽
E. 湿性坏疽

D. 干性坏疽

A small portion of the left lobe of a man's liver is removed because of trauma. Several weeks later, a CT scan of the abdomenshows that the liver has nearly regained its size prior to the injury. Which of the following processes best explains this CT scan finding?
A. Hypertrophy
B. Hyperplasia
C. Fatty change
D. Hydropic change
E. Dysplasia
【参考答案】 B

An old man died subbenly and at autopsy multiple tissue sites were sampled for microscopic analysis. Examination of the tissues showed noncrystalline amorphous deposits of calcium salts in the intestinal mucosa, renal interstitium, and alveolar walls of lungs. Which of the following conditions is most likely to explain these findings?
A. Pulmonary emphysema
B. Generalized atherosclerosis
C. Disseminated tuberculosis
D. Chronic hepatitis
E. Chronic glomerulonephritis
【参考答案】 E

A renal biopsy is performed in a renal failure patient, and the specimen is examined using electron microscopy. Which of the following morphologic changes most clearly suggests a diagnosis of acute tubular necrosis?
A. Mitochondrial swelling
B. Plasma membrane blebs
C. Chromatin clumping
D. Nuclear fragmentation
E. Ribosomal disaggregation
【参考答案】 D

After thrombolytic therapy (溶血栓疗法) with tissue plasminogen activator (t-PA) (血浆纤溶酶原激活剂), the serum creatine kinase (CK,血清肌酸磷酸激酶) level increased in a patient with acute myocardial infarction. Which of the following events most likely occurred following t-PA therapy?
A. Chemical injury
B. Increased synthesis of CK
C. Reperfusion injury
D. Cellular regeneration
E. Myofiber atrophy
【参考答案】 C

An old man has had difficulty with urination (排尿) for the past 10 years. A B ultrasound examination reveals that the prostate gland is enlarged to about twice normal size. The microscopic appearance of the prostate biopsy shows nodules of glands with intervening stroma. Which of the following pathologic processes has most likely occurred in the prostate?
A. Metaplasia
B. Dysplasia
C. Fatty change
D. Hypertrophy
E. Hyperplasia
【参考答案】 E

An old man experienced an episode of chest pain prior to sudden death. A histologic section of left ventricular my-ocardium taken at autopsy showed a deeply eosinophilic staining area with loss of nuclei and cross striations in myocardial fibers. There was no hemorrhage or inflammation. Which of the following conditions most likely produced these myocardial changes?
A. Antibodies directed against myocardium
B. Viral infection
C. Blunt chest trauma
D. Coronary artery thrombosis
E. Protein-deficient diet
【参考答案】 D

Which of the following findings would most likely appear in a liver biopsy specimen from a congenital anemia patient who has required multiple transfusions of RBCs for many years?

B. Bilirubin in canaliculi
C. Hemosiderin in hepatocytes
D. Amyloid in portal triads
E. Steatosis in hepatocytes

女性, 52岁, 书店经理。因近四周来疲乏无力、嗜睡、体重下降、间歇性发热出汗而入院。查体, 左结膜下出血, 两下肢多处出血斑。两手甲下可见线状出血, 脾脏可触及, 心率缓慢, 心前区可闻收缩期吹风样杂音。临床诊断为急性细菌性心内膜炎。患者多脏器可发生栓塞, 其栓子为
A. 血栓栓子
B. 脂肪栓子
C. 细菌栓子
D. 肿瘤栓子
E. 空气栓塞
【参考答案】 C

男性, 25岁, 静脉吸毒者。因突发高热、寒战、皮肤淤点及左前臂注射部位红肿、热、痛二天而住院。查体, 患者体弱、消瘦, 左前臂红肿, 压痛, 针眼处可见脓庖, 皮肤多处淤点。肝脾大, 心前区可闻高调收缩期杂音。血液检查, 白细胞总数25×109/L。以中性粒为主。临床诊断为急性感染性心内膜炎。患者二尖瓣疣状赘生物脫落的栓子引起感染为
A. 肺脏感染

D. 肾脏感染

## 炎症
关于炎症的阐述,错误的是
A. 炎症是白细胞吞噬及清除有害因子的反应
B. 炎症是机体对于损伤因子的防御反应
C. 炎症是机体损伤及抗损伤过程
D. 炎症的局部反应为红、肿、热、痛和功能障碍
E. 炎症的全身反应包括发热、和白细胞计数升高
【参考答案】 A

最常见的致炎因子是
A. 物理性因子
B. 化学性因子
C. 生物性因子
D. 机械性因子
E. 免疫性因子

E. 消化道疾患的儿童

A. 变性
B. 坏死
C. 渗出
D. 充血
E. 增生

B. 充血

D. 间质增生
E. 实质增生

E. 肥大细胞
【参考答案】 C

C. 中性粒细胞

E. 肥大细胞

B. 有活跃的运动能力

E. 有丰富的中性颗粒

B. 可产生肝素
C. 可产生组织胺
D. 可产生5-羟色胺
E. 具有吞噬功能
【参考答案】 E

E. 引起疼痛
【参考答案】 E

不符合化学趋化作用的描述是

D. 可引起发热

D. 败血症

A. 可出现高热、寒战

B. 菌血症

D. 脓毒败血症
E. 病毒血症

B. 高热寒战

D. 皮肤、粘膜点状出血
E. 脾肿大

B. 痈
C. 蜂窝织炎
D. 淋巴管炎
E. 丹毒

A. 急性单纯性阑尾炎
B. 急性蜂窝织炎性阑尾炎
C. 急性蜂窝织炎性阑尾炎伴阑尾周围脓肿
D. 慢性阑尾炎
E. 阑尾粘液囊肿

A. 蜂窝织炎
B. 脓肿
C. 积脓
D. 表面化脓
E. 痈

A. 大量红细胞
B. 大量单核淋巴细胞
C. 大量中性粒细胞
D. 大量清亮液体
E. 大量浆细胞
【参考答案】 C

Staphylococcus aureus organisms are innoculated into the tissues and the immunologic response is observed over the next 24 hours. Which of the following functions is most likely to be served by these lymphatics to produce a specific immune response to these organisms?
A. Carry lymphocytes to peripheral tissue sites
B. Transport antigen presenting cells
C. Remove extravascular tissue fluid
D. Serve as a route for dissemination of infection
E. Provide an emigration point for neutrophils
【参考答案】 B

A 43-year-old woman has had nausea with vomiting persisting for the past 5 weeks. On physical examination there are no abnormal findings. She undergoes an upper GI endoscopy and gastric biopsies are obtained. The microscopic appearance of these biopsies shows mucosal infiltration by lymphocytes, macrophages, and plasma cells. Which of the following conditions is this woman most likely to have?
A. Staphylococcus aureus septicemia
B. Ingestion of chili peppers
C. Diabetes mellitus
D. Tuberculosis
E. Infection with Helicobacter pylori
【参考答案】 E

In an experiment, Enterobacter cloacae organisms are added to a solution containing leukocytes. Engulfment and phagocytosis of the microbes is observed. Next a substance is added which enhances engulfment. Which of the following substances is most likely to produce this effect?
A. Complement C3b
B. Glutathione peroxidase
C. Immunoglobulin M
D. P-selectin
E. NADPH oxidase
【参考答案】 A

A 72-year-old woman did not get a "flu" shot in the fall as recommended for older persons. In the wintertime, she became ill, as did many people in her community, with a respiratory illness that lasted for several weeks. During this illness, she had a fever with a non-productive cough, mild chest pain, myalgias, and headache. What was her chest radiograph most likely to have shown during this illness?
A. 6cm left hilar mass
B. Bilateral interstitial infiltrates
C. Marked hilar lymphadenopathy
D. Left upper lobe consolidation
E. Extensive pleural thickening with pleural effusions
【参考答案】 B

Within minutes following a bee sting, a 37-year-old man develops marked respiratory stridor with dyspnea and wheezing. He also develops swelling and erythema in his arms and legs. An injec-tion of epinephrine helps to reverse these events and he recovers within minutes. Which of the following chemical mediators is most important in the pathogenesis of this man's condition?
A. Bradykinin
B. Complement C5a
C. Nitric oxide
D. Histamine
E. Tumor necrosis factor
【参考答案】 D

A 45-year-old man has been working hard all day long carrying loads of bricks to build a wall. He can use a non-steroidal anti-inflammatory drug such as ibuprofen in order to try and reduce which of the following processes in his arms?
A. Thrombosis
B. Liquefactive necrosis
C. Pain
D. Fibrinolysis
E. Scar formation
【参考答案】 C

A 15-year-old girl has had episodes of sneezing with watery eyes and runny nose for the past 2 weeks. On physical examination she has red, swollen nasal mucosal surfaces. She has had similar episodes each spring and summer when the amount of ragweed pollen in the air is high. Her symptoms are most likely to be mediated by the release of which of the following chemical mediators?
A. Complement C3b
B. Platelet activating factor (PAF)
C. Tumor necrosis factor (TNF)
D. Histamine
E. Immunoglobulin G
【参考答案】 D

A 55-year-old man has a history of hypercholesterolemia with coronary artery disease and suffered a myocardial infarction 2 years ago. He now presents with crushing substernal chest pain. Which of the following laboratory tests will be most useful in this patient?
A. Increased white blood cell count
B. Elevated sedimentation rate
C. Decreased serum complement
D. Increased serum creatine kinase
E. Decreased platelet count
【参考答案】 D

A 36-year-old woman had bilateral silicone breast implants placed two years ago. After that time, she noted increased firmness with slight deformity of the breast on the left. The implants were removed, and there was evidence for leakage of the implant contents on the left. Which of the following cell types is most likely to be characteristic of the inflammatory response in this situation?
A. Mast cell
B. Eosinophil
C. Plasma cell
D. Neutrophil
E. Giant cell
【参考答案】 E

A 45-year-old woman has had a chronic, non-productive cough for 3 months, together with intermittent fever. She has a chestradiograph that reveals multiple small parenchymal nodules along with hilar and cervical lymphadenopathy. A cervical lymph node biopsy is performed. Microscopic examination of the biopsy shows noncaseating granulomatous inflammation. Cultures for bacterial, fungal, and mycobacterial organisms are negative. Which of the following chemical mediators is most important in the development of this inflammation?
A. Interferon gamma
B. Bradykinin
C. Complement C5a
D. Histamine
E. Prostaglandin E2
【参考答案】 A

A 19-year-old woman is in the early second trimester at 16 weeks gestation with her first pregnancy. On a routine prenatal visit, the nurse cannot detect fetal heart tones and an ultrasound reveals no fetal cardiac activity or fetal movement. The mother has noted no recent illness or other factors to account for this fetal demise. She is given a pharmacologic agent to induce abortion. By which of the following mechanisms is this induction most likely to be accomplished?
A. Leukotriene-induced neutrophil aggregation
B. Complement-induced hemolysis
C. Histamine-induced edema
D. Prostaglandin-induced muscular contraction
E. Endotoxin-induced disseminated intravascular coagulation
【参考答案】 D

A 17-year-old driving a pickup truck is involved in a collision. He incurs blunt force abdominal trauma. In response to this injury, cells in tissues of the abdomen are stimulated to enter the G1 phase of the cell cycle from the Go phase. Which of the following cell types is most likely to remain in Go following this injury?
A. Smooth muscle
B. Skeletal muscle
C. Endothelium
D. Fibroblast
E. Hepatocyte
【参考答案】 B

A 37-year-old man has had nausea and vomiting for 5 weeks. He experienced an episode of hematemesis yesterday. On physical examination he has no abnormal findings. Upper GI endoscopy is performed, and there is a 1.5 cm diameter lesion in the gastric antrum which appears to be an area with loss of the epithelial surface. These findings are most typical for which of the following pathologic processes?
A. Abscess
B. Serositis
C. Granuloma
D. Gangrene
E. Ulcer
【参考答案】 E

An inflammatory process that has continued for 3 months includes the transformation of tissue macrophages to epithelioid cells. There are also lymphocytes present. Over time, fibroblasts lay down collagen as the focus of inflammation heals. These events are most likely to occur as an inflammatory response to which of the following infectious agents?
A. Pseudomonas aeruginosa
B. Mycobacterium leprae
C. Cytomegalovirus
D. Giardia lamblia
E. Treponema pallidum
【参考答案】 B

A small sliver of wood becomes embedded in the finger of a 25-year-old man. He does not remove it, and over the next 3 days the area around the sliver becomes red, swollen, and tender. Neutrophils migrate into the injured tissue. Expression of which of the following substances on endothelial cells is most instrumental in promoting this inflammatory reaction?
A. Interferon gamma
B. Hageman factor
C. E-selectin
D. Lysozyme
E. Prostacyclin
【参考答案】 C

A 35-year-old woman has been takingacetylsalicylic acid (aspirin) for arthritis for the past 4 years. Her joint pain is temporarily reduced via this therapy. However, radiographs of her knees reveal continuing joint destruction with loss of articular cartilage and joint space narrowing. This pain reduction is most likely to be the result of diminishing which of the following inflammatory responses?
A. Neutrophil chemotaxis by leukotriene B4
B. Prostaglandin-mediated vasodilation
C. Fever resulting from interleukin-1 release
D. Pain resulting from bradykinin generation
E. Anticoagulation by Hageman factor inhibition
【参考答案】 B

A 90-year-old woman has developed a fever and cough over the past 2 days. Staphylococcus aureus is cultured from her sputum. She receives a course of antibiotic therapy. Two weeks later she no longer has a productive cough, but she still has a fever. A chest radiograph reveals a 3 cm rounded density in the right lower lobe whose liquefied contents form a central air-fluid level. There are no surrounding infiltrates. Which of the following is the best description for this outcome of her pneumonia?
A. Hypertrophic scar
B. Bronchogenic carcinoma
C. Regeneration
D. Abscess formation
E. Progression to chronic inflammation
【参考答案】 D

An episode of marked chest pain lasting 4 hours brings a 51-year-old man to the emergency room. He is found to have an elevated serum creatine kinase. An angiogram reveals complete blockage of the left circumflex artery 2 cm from its origin. Which of the following substances would you most expect to be elaborated around the region of tissue damage in the first 3 days as an initial response which will increase myocardial function and promote healing?
A. Histamine
B. Vascular endothelial growth factor
C. Complement C3b
D. Leukotriene B4
E. Immunogloblulin G
【参考答案】 B

Numerous neutrophils are present in a smear of the exudate from the penile urethra. A. Complement C5a
B. Prostaglandin
C. Hageman factor
D. Bradykinin
E. Histamine
【参考答案】 A

A 42-year-old man has developed marked right lower quadrant abdominal pain over the past 1 day. On physical examination there is rebound tenderness to palpation over the right lower quadrant. Laparoscopic surgery is performed, and the appendix is swollen, erythematous, and partly covered by yellowish exudate. C. 介于良性与恶性之间的肿瘤

E. 肿瘤中既有鳞状细胞癌成分, 又有腺癌成分
【参考答案】 E

属于恶性肿瘤的是
A. 纤维瘤
B. 神经母细胞瘤
C. 骨瘤
D. 乳头状瘤
E. 腺瘤
【参考答案】 B

发生于儿童的恶性肿瘤是
A. 肾母细胞瘤
B. 黑色素瘤
C. 骨髓瘤
D. 鳞状细胞癌
E. 腺癌
【参考答案】 E

男性, 70岁, 1年来常有便血, 肠镜检查, 发现距肛门8cm 的肠粘膜有一2cm 大小的肿物, 中心有溃疡。活检诊断为粘液腺癌, 组织学改变是
A. 癌细胞形成腺腔
B. 癌细胞大小较一致
C. 癌细胞分泌大量粘液
D. 癌细胞周围间质丰富
E. 癌细胞周围血管丰富
【参考答案】 C

鳞状细胞癌的主要诊断依据是
A. 发生于鳞状上皮的覆盖部位
B. 呈浸润性生长
C. 癌细胞形成角化珠和细胞间桥
D. 肿瘤呈灰白色、质硬
E. 经淋巴道转移
【参考答案】 C

胃幽门部一般不会出现

B. 鳞状细胞癌
C. 硬癌
D. 胶样癌
E. 髓样癌
【参考答案】 B

不符合乳头状瘤特征是

B. 可发生于皮肤表面
C. 可发生于胃肠道粘膜
D. 呈外生性生长
E. 极少发生恶变
【参考答案】 E

E. 可由乳头状瘤恶变而来
【参考答案】 A

诊断腺癌的组织学依据是

D. 细胞异型性明显,呈腺样排列
E. 核深染

D. 浸润性生长
E. 形成腺样结构
【参考答案】 B

不符合髓样癌特征的是

B. 癌巢呈实体性,无腺腔形成
C. 癌细胞多
D. 可见淋巴细胞浸润
E. 纤维间质丰富
【参考答案】 E

不符合乳腺纤维腺瘤特征的是
A. 是一种良性肿瘤
B. 好发于青年女性
C. 切面灰白、质韧,呈编织状
D. 包膜完整
E. 由分化良好的纤维组织构成
【参考答案】 E

男性,60岁,1年来进行性吞咽困难,日渐加重。胃镜检查,食管下段可见一溃疡型肿物,质地较硬,溃疡周边隆起,底部凹凸不平。活检的病理诊断最可能是

B. 食管腺癌
C. 食管鳞状细胞癌
D. Barrett 食管
E. 食管平滑肌瘤
【参考答案】 C

女性,58岁,停经6年后,阴道不规则出血2个月。妇科检查,子宫体积增大,如孕两个月大。刮宫取子宫内膜送病理,诊断为中分化子宫内膜腺癌。不符合其组织学改变的是
A. 腺体密集、背靠背

E. 可见病理核分裂
【参考答案】 B

女性,50岁,发现背部肿瘤5年,生长缓慢。检查时,肿瘤直径约5cm 大小,可活动,境界清楚。手术切除,肿瘤呈淡黄色,分叶状,包膜完整,质地较软。此结节最可能是

## 心血管疾病
关于风湿病的叙述,不正确的是
A. 是累及全身结缔组织的变态反应性疾病
B. 以心脏病变对患者危害最大
C. 风湿性心内膜炎引起的慢性心瓣膜病
D. 风湿性关节炎常可导致关节畸形
E. 皮下结节和环形红斑对临床诊断风湿病有帮助

关于风湿病的叙述,错误的是
A. 多见于寒冷地区
B. 早期咽拭子培养,溶血性链球菌阳性率高达70%~90%
C. 抗生素的广泛应用,降低了风湿病的发病率
D. 抗O滴度升高提示本病是由于溶血性链球菌直接作用所致
E. 是一种结缔组织病

A. 纤维素样变性
B. 风湿小结

E. 纤维组织增生

A. 关节病变属纤维素性炎
B. 心外膜病变为浆液性纤维素性炎
C. 心肌病
D. 本病与细菌直接感染有关
E. 心内膜形成较大赘生物
【参考答案】 B

女性,13岁,一月前患扁桃腺炎,经治疗痊愈。1周来患者发热,关节肿胀。心尖区闻及舒张期 II~III 级杂音、心包摩擦音。血白细胞升高,血沉加快。抗O滴度升高。符合患者心脏疾病的描述是
A. 亚急性细菌性心内膜炎
B. 二尖瓣狭窄
C. 二尖瓣关闭不全
D. 急性细菌性心内膜炎
E. 赘疣性血栓性心内膜炎
【参考答案】 B

慢性风湿性瓣膜病常见的联合瓣膜损害是
A. 二尖瓣、肺动脉瓣
B. 二尖瓣和三尖瓣
C. 二尖瓣和主动脉瓣
D. 主动脉瓣和肺动脉瓣
E. 主动脉瓣和三尖瓣
【参考答案】 C

急性风湿病最常见的致死原因是
A. 风湿性关节炎
B. 风湿性心脏病
C. 风湿性脑病变
D. 风湿性动脉炎
E. 风湿性肺炎
【参考答案】 B

不符合风湿病病变的描述是
A. 皮肤环形红斑有助于风湿病的诊断
B. 皮下结节有助于风湿病的诊断
C. 小舞蹈症由椎体外系统受累引起
D. Aschoff 小体仅出现于心肌内
E. 皮下结节内可见纤维素样坏死
【参考答案】 D

亚急性细菌性心内膜炎的最常见的病原体是
A. 金黄色葡萄球菌
B. 草绿色链球菌
C. β溶血性链球菌
D. 大肠杆菌
E. 绿脓杆菌
【参考答案】 B

不符合亚急性细菌性心内膜炎的是
A. 由致病力强的细菌引起
B. 发生于已有病变的心瓣膜
C. 瓣膜赘生物较大,污秽,易脱落
D. 多引起非感染性梗死
E. . 皮肤可见 Osler 结节
【参考答案】 A

亚急性细菌性心内膜炎不引起
A. 慢性瓣膜病
B. 局灶性肾小球肾炎
C. 多发性栓塞性小脓肿
D. 紫癜
E. 脾肿大
【参考答案】 C

女性,14岁,室间隔缺损患者。拔牙后1月来,出现发热(37℃~38℃),食欲下降,消瘦、贫血。查体:心前区闻及杂音,心脏轻度增大,皮肤有散在出血点,甲下线状出血。蛋白尿(+)。符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 急性细菌性心内膜炎
B. 亚急性细菌性心内膜炎
C. 赘疣性血栓性心内膜炎
D. 疣状心内膜炎
E. Libman-Sacks 血栓性心内膜炎
【参考答案】 B

女性,14岁,室间隔缺损患者。拔牙后1月来,出现发热(37℃~38℃),食欲下降,消瘦、贫血。查体:心前区闻及杂音,心脏轻度增大,皮肤有散在出血点,甲下线状出血。蛋白尿(+)。符合该患者肾脏疾病的描述是
A. 弥漫性毛细血管增生性肾小球肾炎
B. 新月体性肾小球肾炎
C. 膜性肾小球肾炎
D. 局灶性肾小球肾炎
E. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎

A. 细菌
B. 霉菌
C. 病毒
D. 衣原体
E. 立克次体

C. 质脆、易脱落
D. 内含细菌菌落
E. 大量纤维组织增生

B. 引起肾梗死

D. 引起肺梗死

C. 细菌菌团

B. 慢性瓣膜病
C. 败血症
D. 细动脉壁纤维素样坏死
E. 血管化脓菌栓塞

不符合急性细菌性心内膜炎的是
A. 瓣膜赘生物体积大、污秽、易脱落
B. 常伴有败血症
C. 发生于已有病变的心瓣膜
D. 可引起乳头肌断裂
E. 可引起心瓣膜穿孔,溃疡形成
【参考答案】 C

女性,26岁,既往体健。正常产后1周来,阴道分泌物增多,伴有臭味,近3天来高热、寒战、神志昏迷。白细胞2万/mm3,肝、脾肿大,心脏左心增大,心前区可闻及收缩期杂音。皮肤有多形淤斑,有的可见脓点。符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 毒血症
B. 细菌血症
C. 败血症
D. 脓毒败血症
E. 病毒血症
【参考答案】 D

女性,26岁,既往体健。正常产后1周来,阴道分泌物增多,伴有臭味,近3天来高热、寒战、神志昏迷。白细胞2万/mm³,肝、脾肿大,心脏左心增大,心前区可闻及收缩期杂音。皮肤有多形淤斑,有的可见脓点。不符合该患者心內膜病变的描述是
A. 心內膜赘生物体积大、污秽、松脆
B. 心內膜赘生物内大量细菌
C. 心内膜赘生物由血小板和纤维素构成
D. 可引起瓣膜穿孔
E. 原本健康的瓣膜受累
【参考答案】 C

不符合非感染性內膜炎的描述是

E. 右心室肥大

男性,34岁,中学时期风湿病史。近年来,常感觉乏力。查体:血压100/60mmHg,心前区闻及收缩期杂音 II~III 级,双肺底少许湿音,肝肿大(肋下3cm),下肢轻度可凹性水肿。符合患者疾病的描述是
A. 房间隔缺损
B. 二尖瓣关闭不全
C. 二尖瓣狭窄
D. 主动脉瓣关闭不全
E. 主动脉瓣狭窄
【参考答案】 B

女性,40岁,既往有风湿病史。2年来,经常感觉乏力、心悸。查体:血压100/60mmHg,心前区可闻及舒张期杂音 II~III 级,双肺底少许湿音,肝肿大(肋下3cm),下肢轻度可凹性水肿。符合患者疾病的描述是

男性,60岁,既往无风湿病史。近年来,经常出现心绞痛。查体:血压90/50mmHg,主动脉瓣闻及收缩期吹风样杂音 II~III 级,左心向左向下扩大。符合患者疾病的诊断是

E. 雌激素

D. 钙化灶形成
E. 出血灶形成
【参考答案】 B

女性,50岁,急性腹痛入院。既往有高 血压病史。查体:血压 90/50mmHg,四 肢温度低,肢端色苍白、腹部无明显包 块,无腹肌紧张及反跳痛。超声诊断为 主动脉夹层。可引起主动脉夹层的是

An 18-year-old man was send to emergency room for suddenly collapse. His vital signs include a temperature of 37.1°C, pulse 84/min, respirations 18/min, and blood pressure 80/40mm Hg. Laboratory findings include hemoglobin of 13.56g/dL, platelet count 252,000/mm³, WBC count 7230/mm³, serum glucose 73mg/dL, and creatinine 1.2mg/dL. The total creatine kinase (CK) level is elevated, with a CK-MB fraction of 10%. Which of the following underlying conditions is most likely to be present in this patient?
A. Congenital ventricular septal defect
B. Familial hypercholesterolemia
C. Marfan syndrome
D. Rheumatic fever
E. Down syndrome
【参考答案】 B

A 55-year-old man experienced chest pain with exertion 3 years ago, and an angiogram showed 75% occlusion of his left anterior descending artery. He was prescribed long acting nitrates and beta blockers, and he remained asymptomatic until the past week. He experienced sudden cardiac arrest when he was admitted to hospital yesterday. Which of the following laboratory findings is most characteristic of the disease affecting this patient?
A. High double-stranded DNA autoanti-body titer
B. Positive ANCA determination
C. Increased creatine kinase-MB fraction (CK-MB)
D. Elevated anti-streptolysin O titer
E. Elevated renin level
【参考答案】 C

A 50-year-old man has had increasing dyspnea for the past 2 days. He experiences sudden cardiac arrest and cannot be resuscitated. The light microscopic appearance of the left ventricular free wall at autopsy is shown above. Which of the following is the most likely diagnosis?[图片]
A. Viral myocarditis
B. Acute rheumatic myocarditis
C. Myocardial infarction
D. Hypertrophic cardiomyopathy
E. Septic embolization
【参考答案】 C

A 50-year-old man experiences crushing substernal chest pain one morning. Over the next 4 hours, the pain persists and begins to radiate to his left arm. He becomes diaphoretic and short of breath and finally goes to the hospital in the evening. An elevated serum value in which of the following laboratory tests would be most useful for diagnosis of this patient on admission to the hospital?
A. CK-MB fraction
B. Lipase
C. AST
D. C-reactive protein
E. ALT
【参考答案】 A

A 11-year-old girl who is normally developed has chronic progressive exercise intolerance. Physical examination shows a temperature of 37.1°C, pulse 70/min, respirations 14/min, and blood pressure 100/60 mmHg. A chest radiograph shows cardiomegaly and mild pulmonary edema. An echocardiogram shows severe left ventricular hypertrophy and a prominent interventricular septum. The right ventricle is slightly thickened. During systole, the anterior leaflet of the mitral valve moves into the outflow tract of the left ventricle. The ejection fraction is abnormally high, and the ventricular volume and cardiac output are both low. Which of the following is the most likely cause of the cardiac abnormalities in this patient?
A. Endomyocardial fibrosis
B. Mutations in the P-myosin heavy chain
C. Autoimmunity against myocardial fibers
D. Elevated anti-streptolysin O titer
E. ECHO virus infection
【参考答案】 B

A 28-year-old man was found dead at home. An external examination by the medical examiner showed splinter hemorrhages under the fingernails and no signs of trauma. The gross appearance of the heart at autopsy is shown. Which of the following conditions is most likely to have been the cause of his death?[图片]
A. Acute infective endocarditis
B. Acute rhematic heart disease
C. Acute myocardial infarction
D. Vasculitis
E. Viral myocarditis
【参考答案】 A

A 65-year-old man with a history of diabetes mellitus had chest pain and an elevated ST level 1 year ago. He was treated in the hospital with antiarrhythmic agents for 1 week. An echocardiogram showed an ejection fraction of 28%. He now has markedly reduced exercise tolerance. Diffuse crackles are heard on auscultation of the lungs. The representative gross appearance of the heart is shown in the following figure. Which of the following complications of this disease is the patient most likely to develop?[图片]
A. Infective endocarditis
B. Chronic valvular disease of the heart
C. Hypertrophic cardiomyopathy
D. Constrictive pericarditis
E. Systemic thromboembolism
【参考答案】 E

A 16-year-old girl died suddenly when she just finished a running. She had been healthy all her life and complained only of limited episodes of chest pain in games during the current school year. Which of the following pathologic findings of the heart is the medical examiner most likely to find?
A. Large, friable vegetations with destruction of aortic valve cusps
B. Thickening of the endocardium with white color change
C. Mitral valvular stenosis with left atrial enlargement
D. Haphazardly arranged hypertrophied septal myocytes
E. Extensive myocardial hemosiderin deposition
【参考答案】 D

A 65-year-old man has had progressive dyspnea for the past 2 years. On physical examination, he is afebrile, his pulse is 77/min, respirations 17/min, and extensive rales are heard in all lung fields. An echocardiogram shows that the left ventricular wall is markedly hypertrophied. A chest radiograph shows pulmonary edema and a prominent left-sided heart shadow. Which of the following conditions has most likely produced these findings?
A. Mitral stenosis
B. Centrilobular emphysema
C. Silicosis
D. Systemic hypertension
E. Tricuspid valve regurgitation
【参考答案】 D

For the past 2 and half years, a 55-year-old man has experienced increased fatigue and decreased exercise tolerance. On auscultation, diffuse crackles are audible. The abdomen is distended with a fluid wave, and there is bilateral pitting edema to the knees. A chest radiograph shows pulmonary edema and marked cardiomegaly. An echocardiogram shows mild tricuspid and mitral regurgitation and reduced right and left ventricular wall motion, with an ejection fraction of 30%. Over the next year he suffers cerebral, renal, and splenic infarctions, and also has infarctions of two toes of his right foot. Which of the following is most likely to have produced these findings?
A. Rheumatic heart disease
B. Hypertrophic cardiomyopathy
C. Dilated cardiomyopathy
D. Coronary atherosclerosis
E. Restrictive cardiomyopathy
【参考答案】 C

A 57-year-old man has a sudden onset of severe substernal chest pain that radiates to the neck. He has tachycardia, hyperventilation, and hypotension on physical examination. No cardiac murmurs are heard on auscultation. Emergent coronary angiography shows a thrombotic occlusion of the left circumflex artery with areas of 60% to 75% narrowing in the proximal circumflex and anterior descending arteries. Which of the following complications of this disease is likely to occur in the future?
A. Myocardial rupture
B. Thromboembolism
C. ventricular aneurysm
D. Pericarditis
E. All above
【参考答案】 E

A 17-year-old man has had a low-grade fever for 3 weeks. On physical examination, his temperature is 38.5 °C, pulse 104/min, respirations 28/min, and blood pressure of 97/60 mm Hg. His spleen tip is palpable and tender. There are also tender hemorrhagic nodules on the palms and soles. A heart murmur is heard on auscultation. Which of the following infectious agents is most likely to be cultured from this patient's blood?
A. Coxsackievirus B
B. Streptococcus viridans group
C. Staphylococci
D. Mycobacterium tuberculosis
E. Candida albicans
【参考答案】 B

A 10-year-old boy had a sore throat and fever 3 weeks ago, and a throat culture was positive for group A b-hemolytic Streptococcus. He is afebrile. On auscultation, a murmur due to mitral regurgitation is audible, and there are diffuse rales over both lungs. Over the next 2 days he has several episodes of atrial fibrillation accompanied by signs of acute left ventricular failure. Which of the following pathologic changes which occurred in this child's heart during hospitalization is most likely to be the cause of the left ventricular failure?
A. Fibrinous pericarditis
B. Fibrosis of mitral valve with fusion of commissures
C. Myocarditis
D. Verrucous endocarditis
E. Amyloidosis
【参考答案】 C

A 62 year-old man experiences episodes of severe substernal chest pain every time he performs a task that requires moderate exercise. The episodes have become more frequent and severe over the past year, but they can be relieved by sublingual nitroglycerin. On physical examination, there are no murmurs or gallops. A chest radiograph shows the lungs are clear and the heart size is nearly normal. Laboratory studies show the total serum cholesterol is 223mg/dL. Which of the following cardiac lesions is most likely to be present?
A. Rheumatic mitral stenosis
B. Coronary atherosclerosis
C. Restrictive cardiomyopathy
D. Viral myocarditis
E. Pericardial tamponade
【参考答案】 B

## 呼吸系统疾病
最常见的慢性阻塞性肺病是
A. 支气管扩张
B. 支气管哮喘
C. 慢性支气管炎
D. 矽肺
E. 慢性空洞性肺结核
【参考答案】 C

不符合慢性支气管炎的描述是
A. 可引起肺心病
B. 可引起肺气肿
C. 吸烟是常见的原因
D. 可伴喘息症状
E. 支气管呈囊性扩张
【参考答案】 E

与慢性支气管炎发病无关的是
A. 呼吸道反复病毒感染
B. 肺孢子虫感染
C. 呼吸道细菌感染
D. 大气污染
E. 吸烟
【参考答案】 B

慢性支气管炎的临床表现是
A. 慢性咳嗽
B. 咳铁锈色痰
C. 肺实变
D. 最大通气量明显降低
E. 大咯血
【参考答案】 A

慢性支气管炎的主要病变不包括
A. 粘膜上皮纤毛脱落、倒伏
B. 肉芽肿形成
C. 粘液腺肥大
D. 管壁淋巴细胞、浆细胞浸润
E. 鳞状上皮化生
【参考答案】 B

慢性支气管炎患者通气功能障碍的病理基础是
A. 粘液腺肥大、增生
B. 上皮纤毛倒伏、脱落
C. 软骨变性萎缩
D. 小气道阻塞
E. 管壁平滑肌萎缩
【参考答案】 D

女性, 50岁, 慢性咳嗽, 咳白色泡沫粘痰, 每年秋冬发作, 持续三个月左右。不符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 可发生肺气肿
B. 可发生支气管肺炎
C. 可发生肺源性心脏病
D. 可发生支气管扩张
E. 可发生鳞状上皮化生
【参考答案】 D

女性, 50岁, 慢性咳嗽, 咳白色泡沫粘痰, 每年秋冬发作, 持续三个月左右。不符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 支气管粘膜上皮变性坏死
B. 支气管粘膜上皮杯状细胞增多
C. 支气管粘液腺肥大
D. 支气管软骨变性萎缩
E. 支气管壁弹力纤维增生
【参考答案】 E

不符合急性细支气管炎的特点是
A. 累及管径<2mm 的细支气管
B. 常见于婴幼儿
C. 多由流感杆菌、葡萄球菌等细菌感染引起155
D. 多引起喘憋性呼吸困难
E. 可引起呼吸衰竭
【参考答案】 C

急性细支气管炎出现呼吸困难的主要病变基础是
A. 细支气管上皮坏死脱落
B. 细支气管管腔内炎性渗出物阻塞
C. 肺间质炎细胞浸润
D. 肺萎陷
E. 急性阻塞性肺气肿
【参考答案】 B

不符合支气管哮喘的描述是
A. 属Ⅰ型超敏反应性疾病
B. 有多种炎症介质参与
C. 支气管内大量粘液分泌
D. 支气管壁多量嗜碱粒细胞浸润
E. 支气管平滑肌痉挛
【参考答案】 D

引起支气管哮喘的主要原因是
A. 支气管粘膜内杯状细胞增多
B. 支气管粘膜水肿
C. 支气管壁平滑肌痉挛
D. 支气管腔内粘液增多
E. 支气管管壁炎细胞浸润
【参考答案】 C

符合支气管哮喘的描述是
A. 单基因遗传病
B. 常染色体显性遗传病
C. 常染色体隐性遗传病
D. 多因素遗传病
E. 性连锁遗传病
【参考答案】 D

不符合支气管哮喘的描述是
A. 多种炎细胞参与
B. 多种炎症介质参与
C. 多种细胞因子参与
D. 嗜酸粒细胞是最主要的效应细胞
E. 气道的高反应性是重要特征
【参考答案】 D

不符合支气管哮喘病变的描述是
A. 可见胆固醇结晶
B. 可见 Curschmann 螺旋
C. 支气管内粘液栓填塞
D. 支气管壁多量炎细胞浸润
E. 支气管平滑肌肥厚
【参考答案】 A

不符合支气管哮喘的合并症的描述是
A. 可引起肺气肿
B. 可引起肺炎
C. 可引起肺心病
D. 可引起支气管扩张
E. 不引起急性死亡
【参考答案】 E

引起肺气肿的因素有
A. 小气道的狭窄、阻塞
B. 肺泡壁破坏、弹性减弱
C. 弹性蛋白酶增多
D. 遗传性 a₁-抗胰蛋白酶缺乏
E. 以上都是
【参考答案】 E

腺泡中央型肺气肿病变主要累及
A. 肺泡管
B. 终末细支气管
C. 肺泡囊
D. 呼吸性细支气管
E. 细支气管
【参考答案】 D

累及肺腺泡各部分的肺气肿为
A. 腺泡中央型
B. 全腺泡型
C. 腺泡周围型
D. 间质性肺气肿
E. 不规则型
【参考答案】 B

在小叶间隔与肺膜连接处形成串珠状156小气泡, 应诊断为
A. 小叶周围型
B. 不规则型
C. 肺大泡
D. 间质性肺气肿
E. 全小叶型
【参考答案】 D

肺气肿可导致
A. 肺源性心脏病
B. 自发性气胸
C. 肺性脑病
D. 筒状胸
E. 以上都是
【参考答案】 E

肺气肿的病变累及
A. 肺泡
B. 终末细支气管
C. 呼吸性细支气管
D. 肺泡管
E. 以上都是
【参考答案】 E

全腺泡型肺气肿的主要原因是
A. 小气道阻塞
B. 支气管壁炎细胞浸润
C. 肺组织炎细胞浸润
D. 先天性 α₁-抗胰蛋白酶缺乏
E. 支气管壁扩张
【参考答案】 D

女性, 60岁, 慢性支气管炎近10年,近3年来感觉气短、胸闷、呼吸困难。查体: 桶状胸, 胸廓呼吸运动减弱;叩诊呈过清音, 心浊音界缩小, 肝浊音界下降; 听诊呼吸音减弱。不符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 肺泡扩张
B. 肺泡间隔变窄
C. 肺泡融合成囊泡状
D. 肺间质广泛纤维化
E. 肺组织膨胀
【参考答案】 D

下列疾病中, 最常引起肺心病者是
A. 肺动脉栓塞
B. 胸廓畸形
C. 矽肺
D. 慢性肺结核
E. 肺气肿
【参考答案】 E

A. 心脏增大
B. 心尖钝圆
C. 右心室扩张
D. 肺动脉圆锥膨隆
E. 肺动脉下 2cm 处右心室壁厚超过5mm
【参考答案】 E

A. 心肌细胞肥大, 核大深染
B. 心肌细胞增生, 数目增多
C. 心肌纤维萎缩、变细
D. 心肌细胞肌浆溶解
E. 心肌间质纤维化

C. 无肌性细动脉肌化
D. 小动脉弹力纤维增生
E. 肌型小动脉中膜肥厚

A. 小气道阻塞

D. 肺间质纤维组织增生

C. 肺结核
D. 肺脓肿
E. 早期肺门淋巴结病变

B. 支气管旁淋巴组织增生

A. 各种致炎因子引起的肺的炎症
B. 各种细菌引起的肺的炎症
C. 各种原因引起的肺泡的炎症
D. 各种原因引起肺的急性渗出性炎症
E. 以中性白细胞渗出为主的肺的炎症

E. 真菌

D. 病毒多见

A. 由多种致病菌引起
B. 常为其他疾病的合并症

C. 肺泡的纤维素性炎
D. 肺泡出血性炎

A. 主要由病毒引起
B. 小叶和小叶融合性病变
C. 病变以肺的背部和下部较重
D. 可导致心力衰竭
E. 可引起肺脓肿

B. 病变从细支气管开始

D. 常咳铁锈色痰
E. 可破坏肺泡壁

小叶性肺炎一般不引起
A. 肺肉质变
B. 脓胸
C. 脓毒败血症
D. 支气管扩张
E. 肺脓肿

不属于小叶性肺炎的是
A. 麻疹后肺炎
B. 病毒性肺炎
C. 手术后肺炎
D. 吸入性肺炎
E. 坠积性肺炎
【参考答案】 B

男性, 80岁, 直肠癌根治术后2周。7天来发热(39℃)、咳嗽、咳粘液脓痰。查体: 双肺下叶背侧可闻及湿罗音。X线见双肺下叶散在不规则斑片状模糊阴影。符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 肺泡纤维素性炎
B. 支气管肺泡化脓性炎
C. 间质性肺炎
D. 病毒性肺炎
E. 支原体性肺炎
【参考答案】 B

男性, 80岁, 直肠癌根治术后2周。7天来发热(39℃)、咳嗽、咳粘液脓痰。查体: 双肺下叶背侧可闻及湿罗音。X线见双肺下叶散在不规则斑片状模糊阴影。不符合该患者疾病的合并症的描述是
A. 中毒性休克
B. 肺脓肿
C. 脓胸
D. 肺栓塞
E. 心力衰竭
【参考答案】 D

大叶性肺炎的病变属于159
A. 化脓性炎
B. 出血性炎
C. 纤维素性炎
D. 浆液性炎
E. 肉芽肿性炎
【参考答案】 C

大叶性肺炎发生肺肉质变是因为
A. 局部组织缺血
B. 纤维素渗出过多
C. 机体抵抗力降低
D. 吞噬细胞数量少或功能障碍
E. 合并其他细菌感染
【参考答案】 D

不符合大叶性肺炎的描述是
A. 多由肺炎链球菌感染引起
B. 累及整个或多个大叶
C. 属于纤维素性炎
D. 破坏小支气管壁和肺泡壁结构
E. 咳铁锈色痰
【参考答案】 D

大叶性肺炎的合并症有
A. 肺肉质变
B. 感染性休克
C. 纤维素性胸膜炎
D. 肺脓肿
E. 以上都是
【参考答案】 E

A. 败血症
B. 肺脓肿
C. 脓胸
D. 感染性休克
E. 以上都是
【参考答案】 E

不符合大叶性肺炎病理变化的描述是
A. 肺泡腔内浆液渗出
B. 肺泡腔内纤维素渗出
C. 肺泡腔内可有红细胞
D. 肺泡腔内中性粒细胞渗出
E. 肺泡腔内多量心力衰竭细胞
【参考答案】 E

男性, 30岁, 2天前上呼吸道感染, 病情迅速加重, 发热(39.8℃), 伴寒战、胸痛、咳嗽、咳铁锈色痰。X线检查见左肺下叶大片致密阴影。符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 大叶性肺炎
B. 小叶性肺炎
C. 间质性肺炎
D. 病毒性肺炎
E. 支原体性肺炎
【参考答案】 A

男性, 30岁, 2天前上呼吸道感染, 病情迅速加重, 发热(39.8℃), 伴寒战、胸痛、咳嗽、咳铁锈色痰。X线检查见左肺下叶大片致密阴影。不符合该患者疾病的描述是

不符合放线菌病的描述是
A. 主要通过外源性感染
B. 常引起慢性化脓性炎
C. 脓液中可见“硫磺颗粒”

E. 病变可累及颌面部、胸部和腹部
【参考答案】 A

女性,60岁,40年吸烟史。慢性咳嗽30年,近1月来痰中带血丝。X线检查,显示左肺门处不规则巨大阴影。支气管镜检查,左主支气管粘膜不平,管腔狭窄。活检病理诊断最可能是
A. 肺结核病
B. 肺结节病
C. 细支气管肺泡细胞癌
D. 肺鳞状细胞癌
E. 肺炎性假瘤
【参考答案】 D

女性,50岁,本人不吸烟,但家中多个成员吸烟。查体偶然发现右肺近胸膜处有一直径3cm 的边界较清的阴影,伴肺门淋巴结肿大。该结节最可能是
A. 鳞状细胞癌
B. 细支气管肺泡细胞癌
C. 腺癌
D. 大细胞癌
E. 小细胞癌
【参考答案】 C

男性,60岁,3个月来咳嗽、胸痛。X线检查,左肺下叶大片云雾状、不均匀密度增高阴影,经用抗生素治疗无效。痰脱落细胞检查,可见腺癌细胞。对该患者疾病的诊断是

D. 小细胞癌

B. 直径多在 1cm以内
C. 易发生癌变
D. 易合并穿孔
E. 易发生出血
【参考答案】 C

A. 胃酸分泌增多
B. 胃泌素瘤
C. 胃窦部G细胞增多
D. 迷走神经兴奋性降低
E. 胆汁反流
【参考答案】 D

D. 不易发生出血
E. 不易发生穿孔

B. 幽门狭窄
C. 癌变
D. 出血
E. 粘膜萎缩
【参考答案】 E

女性, 48岁, 10多年来, 反酸、嗳气、上腹部饱胀, 伴有规律性上腹痛, 空腹发作, 夜间更重, 进食可缓解。符合该患者疾病的诊断是

D. 胃癌
E. 慢性胆囊炎

不符合大肠癌转移的描述是
A. 可直接蔓延至邻近器官
B. 可经淋巴道转移到结肠上、旁、中间和末端淋巴结

男性, 60岁, 半年来大便带血。结肠镜检查, 距肛门 10cm 处可见一溃疡形肿物, 约3cm×5cm 大小, 呈火山口状, 底部凹凸不平。符合该肿物的病理学改变是
A. 肠粘膜腺体正常
B. 肠粘膜慢性炎
C. 腺体密集、腺上皮细胞异型性明显
D. 肉芽肿形成
E. 乳头形成、乳头被覆上皮轻度异型性
【参考答案】 C

男性, 62岁, 直肠癌切除标本, 病理检查为低分化腺癌, 浸及肠壁深肌层, 肠系膜淋巴结无转移。其分期为
A. Dukes A1 期
B. Dukes A2 期
C. Dukes B期
D. Dukes C期
E. Dukes D期

Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy reveals an ulcerative mass in the mid-esophagus which almost obstructs the entire lumen. Biopsy is performed and microscopic examination confirms the presence of a squamous cell carcinoma. Which of the following statements is most likely correct?
A. The tumor is restricted to the submucosa
B. The progression is excellent
C. The tumor has metastasized via lymph vessels
D. The precursor lesion is Barrett's esophagus with dysplasia
E. None of above
【参考答案】 D

A 53-year-old male presents with increasing dysphagia and retrosternal pain. He has also noticed a 7-kg weight loss over the past 3 months. On physical examination, there are no remarkable findings. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy reveals a 4 cm length of narrowing of the mid-esophagus lined by coarse mucosa. Esophagectomy is performed and microscopic examination confirms the presence of an advanced stage carcinoma. Which of the following appropriately describes the gross appearance of this carcinoma?
A. Polypoid pattern
B. Fungating pattern
C. Ulcerative pattern
D. Infiltrative pattern
E. Depressed pattern
【参考答案】 D

A 55-year-old man has had dysphagia for 6 months. On physical examination there are no abnormal findings. He undergoes an upper GI endoscopy, and a small shal-low ulcer is noted in the middle portion of the esophagus. Biopsies from this area show a moderately differentiated squamous cell carcinoma. Esophagectomy is performed. The microscopic changes are as shown above and no metastases are found. Which of the following interpretations is most applicable to his neoplasm?[图片]
A. It has a high potential to metastasize to the liver
B. The incidence is high in Western countries
C. The 5-year survival following resection is >80%
D. It is associated with a high fruit diet
E. Its precursor lesion is Barrett's esophagus
【参考答案】 D

A 58-year-old man has had increasing difficulty swallowing for the past 6 months. He has lost 5 kg in the past 2 months. No abnormal physical examination findings are noted. Upper GI endoscopy reveals a nearly circumferential mass with overlying ulceration in the mid esophageal region. Biopsy of the mass reveals pink polygonal cells with marked hyperchromatism and pleomorphism. Which of the following is the most likely risk factor for development of his disease?
A. Iron deficiency
B. Reflux esophagitis
C. Helicobacter pylori infection
D. Ice-cold beverage or food
E. Chronic alcoholism
【参考答案】 E

A 50-year-old male farmer from Henan province presents with increasing dysphagia and has noticed a 6kg weight loss over the past 2 months. His grandfather and father both died in their late 50's with similar symptoms. On physical examination, there are no remarkable findings. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy shows a 4cm ulcerative mass in the mid-esophagus that occupies 1/2 of the esophageal lumen. Esophagectomy is performed, and the gross appearance of the lesion is shown in the preceding figure. Which of the following is most likely to be seen on microscopic sections of this mass?[图片]
A. Columnar epithelium with goblet cells
B. Adenocarcinoma
C. Dense collagenous scar
D. Squamous cell carcinoma
E. Ectatic vascular channels
【参考答案】 D

A 58-year-old man has had sour brash and substernal pain after meals for many years. For the past year, he has had increased dysphagia even when drinking liquids. On physical examination, there are no remarkable findings. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy shows a lower esophageal mass that nearly occludes the lumen of the esophagus. Biopsy of this mass is most likely to show which of the following neoplasms?
A. Malignant neoplasm which forms glandular structure
B. Malignant neoplasm which is composed of spindle cells
C. Malignant neoplasm which forms "keratin pearls"
D. Malignant neoplasm which has "Reed-Sternberg" cells
E. Malignant neoplasm which has neuroendocrine granules within the cytoplasm
【参考答案】 A

A 49-year-old man has complained of "heartburn" after meals for the past decade. There are no remarkable findings on physical examination. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy is performed, and a biopsy specimen is taken above the gastroesophageal junction. Microscopically, the mucosa shows columnar metaplasia with goblet cells. Which of the following description is not correct for this disease?
A. It is caused by gastroesophageal reflux
B. The diagnosis for this condition is Barrett's esophagus
C. The morphologic changes referred to as squamous metaplasia
D. The gross appearance of the lesion is usually velvety and erythematous
E. The patient is at risk for developing adenocarcinoma
【参考答案】 C

A 49-year-old man has had progressive difficulty on swallowing during the past 6 months. No significant findings are observed on physical examination. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy shows areas of velvety erythematous mucosa above the Z-line. A biopsy specimen from the lower esophagus has the microscopic appearance shown in the preceding figure. Which of the following is the most likely consequence of this disease?[图片]
A. Esophageal stricture
B. Adenocarcinoma
C. Hematemesis
D. Esophageal laceration
E. Squamous cell carcinoma
【参考答案】 B

A 57-year-old woman has had burning epigastric pain after meals for more than 1 year. Physical examination shows no abnormal findings. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy shows an erythematous patch in the lower esophageal mucosa. A biopsy specimen shows basal squamous epithelial hyperplasia, elongation of lamina propria papillae, and scattered intraepithelial neutrophils with some eosinophils. As a consequence of this patient's condition, which of the following complications is not likely to occur?
A. Intestinal metaplasia
B. Barrett's esophagus
C. Squamous cell carcinoma
D. Esophageal stricture
E. Adenocarcinoma
【参考答案】 C

A 35-year-old woman has experienced "heartburn" and sour regurgitation following meals for the past 2 years. There are no abnormal findings on physical examination. Upper GI endoscopy is performed. There are 2 to 3 cm long tongues of erythematous mucosa extending from the gastroesophageal junction at the Z line upward into the lower esophagus. Biopsies of this region are performed and the microscopic pictures is shown above. Which of the following interpretations is most appropriate for this woman's findings?[图片]
A. She has a congenital anomaly
B. Her risk for squamous cell carcinoma is increased
C. Her consumption of alcohol is high
D. She has chronic gastroesophageal reflux
E. Formation of a diverticulum may occur

A 28-year-old HIV positive woman has had pain on swallowing for the past week. No abnormal physical examination findings are noted. Upper GI endoscopy is performed. There are multiple small ulcers in the lower esophagus. Inclusions are found in the biopsies from these ulcers. She is most likely to have infection with which of the following organisms?
A. Helicobacter pylori
B. Candida albicans
C. Aspergillus
D. Mycobacterium avium-complex
E. Cytomegalovirus
【参考答案】 E

A 16-year-old boy sees the physician because he has had pain for 1 week whenhe swallows food. Physical examination shows no abnormal findings. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy shows 0.5- to 0.8- cm mucosal ulcers in the region of the middle to lower esophagus. The shallow ulcers are round, sharply demarcated, and have an erythematous base. The boy has a history of acute lymphoblastic leukemia and is receiving chemotherapy. Which of the following is most likely to produce these findings?
A. Helicobacter pylori infection
B. Herpes simplex virus infection
C. Gastroesophageal reflux disease
D. Barrett's esophagus
E. Mycobacterium tuberculosis infection
【参考答案】 B

An 18-year-old woman has had several episodes of aspiration of food associated with difficulty swallowing during the past year. On auscultation, crackles are heard at the base of the right lung. A barium swallow shows marked esophageal dilation above the level of the lower esophageal sphincter. No myenteric ganglia are present in a biopsy specimen from the lower esophagus. Which of the following is the most likely diagnosis?
A. Achalasia
B. Esophageal stricture
C. Barrett's esophagus
D. Reflux esophagitis
E. Hiatal hernia
【参考答案】 A

A 51-year-old man has a sudden onset of massive emesis of bright red blood. On physical examination, his temperature is 36.9°C, pulse 103/min, respirations 19/min, and blood pressure 85/50mm Hg. Laboratory studies show AST 137U/L and ALT 108U/L. The serologic test result for HBsAg is positive. He has had no prior episodes of hematemesis. The hematemesis is most likely to be a consequence of which of the following?
A. Barrett esophagus
B. Esophageal laceration
C. Esophageal varices
D. Reflux esophagitis
E. Squamous cell carcinoma
【参考答案】 C

A 35-year-old man experiences a bout of prolonged vomiting, followed by massive hematemesis. On physical examination in the emergency room, he has vital signs of T 36.7°C, P 108, RR 22, and BP 80/40 mm Hg. His heart has a regular rate and rhythm with no murmurs and his lungs are clear to auscultation. There is no abdominal tenderness or distension and bowel sounds are present. His stool is negative for occult blood. Which of the following is the most likely diagnosis?
A. Hiatal hernia
B. Esophageal laceration
C. Esophageal pulsion diverticulum
D. Reflux esophagitis
E. Esophageal squamous cell carcinoma
【参考答案】 B

## 泌尿系统疾病
肾小球肾炎属于
A. 化脓性炎
B. 纤维素性炎
C. 肉芽肿性炎
D. 出血性炎
E. 非化脓性变态反应性炎
【参考答案】 E

不符合肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 可由循环免疫复合物引起
B. 可由原位免疫复合物引起
C. 可由肾小球固有抗原引起
D. 可由肾小球植入抗原引起
E. 可由细菌感染引起
【参考答案】 E

肾小球肾炎属于
A. I型变态反应
B. II 型变态反应
C. III 型变态反应
D. IV 型变态反应
E. 以上都不是
【参考答案】 C

不符合肾小球的描述是
A. 再生能力强
B. 由毛细血管丛和肾球囊构成
C. 滤过膜由内皮细胞、基底膜和上皮细胞构成
D. 系膜细胞具有吞噬功能
E. 滤过膜带负电荷
【参考答案】 A

非免疫复合物性肾小球肾炎时,引起肾小球损伤的因素是
A. 补体
B. 血小板
C. 中性粒细胞
D. 巨噬细胞
E. T细胞
【参考答案】 E

不属于内源性抗原的是
A. 肿瘤抗原
B. 基底膜抗原
C. 异种血清
D. 核抗原
E. Heymann 抗原
【参考答案】 C

可引起原位免疫复合物性肾小球肾炎的植入性抗原是
A. 基底膜抗原
B. Heymann 抗原
C. 聚合性 IgG
D. 内皮细胞膜抗原
E. 系膜细胞膜抗原
【参考答案】 C

不符合循环免疫复合物引起的肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 由肾小球性抗原引起
B. 由非肾小球性抗原引起
C. 抗原抗体在血液中形成复合物
D. 抗原抗体复合物沉积于肾小球引起炎症
E. 免疫复合物的大小、所带电荷在发病中起重要作用
【参考答案】 A

急性链球菌感染后肾小球肾炎属于
A. 弥漫性毛细血管内增生性肾小球肾炎
B. 弥漫性新月体性肾小球肾炎
C. 弥漫性膜性肾小球肾炎
D. 弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎
E. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎
【参考答案】 A

A. 肾小球球囊壁层上皮细胞
B. 肾小球球囊脏层上皮细胞
C. 肾小球系膜细胞和内皮细胞
D. 肾小球内皮细胞
E. 肾小球系膜细胞
【参考答案】 C

不符合弥漫性毛细血管内增生性肾小球肾炎的是
A. 肾小球内皮细胞增生
B. 肾小球系膜细胞增生
C. 肾小球基底膜弥漫性增厚
D. 肾小球毛细血管壁显示 IgG 和 C3的颗粒荧光
E. 肾小球基底膜外侧有驼峰状电子致密物
【参考答案】 C

不符合弥漫性毛细血管内增生性肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 可引起大红肾

B. 毛细血管基底膜上皮侧见驼峰状致密沉积物
C. 脏层上皮细胞足突融合
D. 毛细血管基底膜严重缺损
E. 毛细血管基底膜內见致密沉积物

B. 血尿
C. 蛋白尿

E. 脓尿

E. 肾小球内无免疫复合物沉积

E. 肾小球基底膜外侧有多数电子致密物

B. 肾小球内皮细胞增生

E. 肾小球毛细血管壁弥漫性增厚
【参考答案】 E

男性, 38岁, 面部及下肢浮肿两月余,尿液检查蛋白++++, 肾穿刺组织光镜观察肾小球体积增大, 肾小球毛细血管基底膜弥漫性增厚。此例肾炎的类型最大可能是
A. 弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎
B. 弥漫性膜性肾小球肾炎
C. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎
D. 弥漫性新月体性肾小球肾炎
E. 弥漫性毛细血管内增生性肾小球肾炎
【参考答案】 B

男性, 38岁, 面部及下肢浮肿两月余,尿液检查蛋白++++, 肾穿刺组织光镜观察肾小球体积增大, 肾小球毛细血管基底膜弥漫性增厚。此例患者肾脏呈
A. 大红肾
B. 固缩肾
C. 大白肾
D. 蚤咬肾
E. 以上都不是
【参考答案】 C

低补体血症性肾小球肾炎属于
A. 弥漫性新月体性肾小球肾炎
B. 慢性硬化性肾小球肾炎
C. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎
D. 弥漫性膜性肾小球肾炎
E. 弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎
【参考答案】 E

符合弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 系膜区致密物沉积
B. 基底膜内致密物沉积
C. 基底膜和上皮细胞间驼峰状致密物沉积
D. 基底膜內和基底膜内、外侧致密物沉积
E. 无致密物沉积
【参考答案】 D

致密物沉积病属于
A. 弥漫性新月体性肾小球肾炎
B. 慢性硬化性肾小球肾炎
C. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎
D. 弥漫性膜性肾小球肾炎
E. 弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎
【参考答案】 E

不符合膜性增生性肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 多由高分子量循环免疫复合物引起
B. 多数患者预后良好
C. IgG 和 C3 沉积于系膜区和基底膜
D. 系膜细胞和基质增生
E. 可引起肾病综合征
【参考答案】 B

符合致密物沉积病的描述是
A. C3 颗粒沉积于基底膜内
B. 患者预后良好
C. 毛细血管基底膜正常
D. IgG 颗粒沉积于基底膜内
E. 临床表现为急性肾炎综合征
【参考答案】 A

弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的主要病理特点是
A. 肾小球系膜细胞和内皮细胞增生
B. 肾小球基底膜弥漫增厚并有钉突形成
C. 肾小球系膜细胞及基质增生
D. 肾小球硬化
E. 肾小球无明显病变
【参考答案】 C

不符合弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的病变是
A. 系膜细胞和基质增生
B. 毛细血管无明显改变
C. 系膜区电子致密物沉积
D. 系膜区 IgM、IgG 和 C3 沉积
E. 系膜细胞和内皮细胞弥漫性增生
【参考答案】 E

符合弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的临床表现是
A. 发病率高
B. 青少年多见
C. 可表现为急性肾炎综合征
D. 可表现为隐匿性肾炎综合征
E. 不表现为肾病综合征
【参考答案】 E

符合弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的电镜下变化是
A. 系膜区致密物沉积
B. 基底膜內致密物沉积
C. 基底膜和上皮细胞间驼峰状致密物沉积
D. 基底膜內和基底膜内、外侧致密物沉积
E. 无致密物沉积
【参考答案】 A

IgA 肾病属于
A. 毛细血管增生性肾小球肾炎
B. 抗基底膜性肾小球肾炎
C. 弥漫性膜增生性肾小球肾炎
D. 弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎
E. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎
【参考答案】 D

不符合IgA肾病的描述是
A. 约占弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的50%
B. 青少年多见
C. 可引起肉眼血尿
D. 预后差
E. 可引起肾病综合征
【参考答案】 D

不符合弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 约占原发性肾小球肾炎的50%
B. 可分为IgA肾病及非 IgA 肾病
C. 部分病例可发展为硬化性肾小球肾炎
D. 皮质激素和细胞毒药物治疗无效
E. 可引起肾病综合征
【参考答案】 D

符合弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 肾小球系膜区免疫复合物沉积
B. 肾小球基底膜內免疫复合物沉积
C. 肾小球基底膜和上皮细胞间驼峰状免疫复合物沉积
D. 肾小球基底膜和内皮细胞间免疫复合物沉积
E. 肾小球内无免疫复合物沉积
【参考答案】 A

轻微病变性肾小球肾炎的主要病理特点是
A. 肾小管上皮脂肪变性和坏死
B. 肾小球系膜细胞增生
C. 肾小球内皮细胞增生
D. 肾小球球囊壁层上皮细胞增生
E. 肾小球球囊脏层上皮细胞足突融合
【参考答案】 E

轻微病变性肾小球肾炎的免疫病理变化是
A. 肾小球基底膜上皮下有免疫复合物沉积
B. 肾小球基底膜內有免疫复合物沉积
C. 肾小球基底膜内皮细胞下有免疫复合物沉积
D. 肾小球系膜区有免疫复合物沉积
E. 以上都不是
【参考答案】 E

不符合轻微病变性肾小球肾炎的临床表现是
A. 高度水肿
B. 血尿
C. 低蛋白血症
D. 高胆固醇血症
E. 大量蛋白尿
【参考答案】 B

B. 属抗基底膜肾小球肾炎
C. 由植入性非肾性抗原引起
D. 通过补体旁路激活引起
E. 由T细胞介导引起
【参考答案】 E

与免疫复合物无关的肾小球肾炎是
A. 弥漫性膜性肾小球肾炎
B. 弥漫性新月体性肾小球肾炎
C. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎
D. 弥漫性毛细血管內增生性肾小球肾炎
E. 弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎
【参考答案】 C

多发生于儿童的肾小球肾炎是

B. 弥漫性膜性肾小球肾炎
C. 弥漫性膜性增生性肾小球肾炎

【参考答案】 E

A. 弥漫性新月体性肾小球肾炎
B. 轻微病变性肾小球肾炎

E. 弥漫性系膜增生性肾小球肾炎

轻微病变性肾小球肾炎常表现为

C. 慢性肾炎综合征
D. 隐匿性肾炎综合征
E. 急进性肾炎综合征
【参考答案】 A

不符合局灶性节段性肾小球肾炎的描述是

B. 肾小球毛细血管袢节段性坏死
C. 可引起血尿
D. 有复发倾向
E. 多引起肾功能衰竭

B. 急性肾炎综合征

【参考答案】 C

A. 由循环免疫复合物引起
B. 属抗基底膜肾小球肾炎
C. 由植入性非肾性抗原引起
D. 病因不明
E. 由T细胞介导引起
【参考答案】 D

A. 部分肾小球受累

C. 肾小球节段性硬化
D. IgM 和 C3 局灶性沉积
E. 基底膜增厚呈双轨状
【参考答案】 E

继发性局灶性节段性肾小球肾炎常表现为
A. 可继发于系统性红斑狼疮
B. 可继发于过敏性紫癜
C. 可继发于细菌性心内膜炎
D. 可继发于结节性动脉炎
E. 不继发于艾滋病
【参考答案】 E

不符合局灶性节段性肾小球肾炎的描述是
A. 不转变为慢性硬化性肾小球肾炎
B. 多数肾小球无病变
C. 多数可自行消退
D. 多数对肾功能影响较小
E. 多数呈隐匿性肾炎综合征
【参考答案】 A

硬化性肾小球肾炎的主要病理特点是
A. 肾间质纤维化
B. 肾小管萎缩
C. 部分肾小球硬化、肾小管萎缩和肾间质纤维化
D. 大部分肾小球硬化、肾小管萎缩和肾间质纤维化
E. 入球小动脉玻璃样变性
【参考答案】 D

不符合硬化性肾小球肾炎的是
A. 部分肾单位代偿肥大
B. 大部分肾单位硬化和萎缩
C. 颗粒性萎缩肾
D. 肾盂扩张
E. 肾间质纤维化

C. 多尿夜尿
D. 管型尿
E. 血尿
【参考答案】 C

很少发展为慢性硬化性肾小球肾炎的是
A. IgA肾病
B. 膜性增生性肾小球肾炎

D. 蚤咬肾
E. 大瘢痕性固缩肾

C. 可由新月体性肾小球肾炎转化而来

B. 肾间质纤维化

膀胱癌最常见的组织学类型是
A. 腺癌
B. 鳞癌
C. 移行细胞癌
D. 未分化癌
E. 腺鳞癌
【参考答案】 C

女性,60岁,送检组织为膀胱肿瘤标本,肉眼检查:肿瘤直径约 3cm,呈乳头状,表面溃疡形成,切面灰白、质硬,侵及膀胱肌层。符合此肿瘤的诊断是
A. 腺癌
B. 鳞癌
C. 移行细胞癌
D. 未分化癌

What is the leading cause of end stage renal disease?
A. Membranous glomerulonephritis
B. Diabetic nephropathy
C. IgA nephropathy
D. Minimal change disease
E. Membranoproliferative glomerulonephritis
【参考答案】 B

A 65-year-old man recently retired after many years in a job that involved exposure to aniline dyes, including β-naphthylamine. One month ago, he had an episode of hematuria that was not accompanied by abdominal pain. On physical examination, there are no abnormal findings. Urinalysis shows 4+hematuria and no ketones, glucose, or protein. Microscopic examination of the urine shows RBCs that are too numerous to count, 5 to 10 WBCs per high-power field, and no crystals or casts. The result of a urine culture is negative. Which of the following is the most likely diagnosis?
A. Renal cell carcinoma
B. Hemorrhagic cystitis
C. Urothelial carcinoma
D. Tubercular cystitis
E. Squamous cell carcinoma of the urethra
【参考答案】 C

A 49-year-old man had increased swelling in the extremities for 2 months. Physical examination showed generalized edema. A 24-hour urine collection yielded 4.1 g of protein as well as albumin and globulins. Extensive testing showed no evidence of any systemic disease, such as diabetes mellitus or systemic lupus erythematosus. He did not respond to a course of corticosteroid therapy. A renal biopsy was performed, and microscopic examination showed diffuse thickening of the basement membrane. Immunofluorescence staining with antibody to the C3 component of complement was positive in a granular pattern in the glomerular capillary loops. Two years later, he experiences increasing malaise, and laboratory studies show serum creatinine level of 45 mg/dL and urea nitrogen level of 44 mg/dL. Which of the following immunologic mechanisms was most likely responsible for the glomerular changes observed in the biopsy specimen?
A. Antibodies that react with basement membrane collagen
B. Antibodies against streptococci that cross-react with the basement membrane
C. Release of cytokines by inflammatory cells
D. Cytotoxic T cells directed against renal antigens
E. Deposition of immune complexes on the basement membrane
【参考答案】 E

One week after a mild flu like illness, a 9-year-old boy has an episode of hematuria that subsides within 2 days. One month later, he tells his parents that his urine is red again. On physical examination, there are no significant findings. Urinalysis shows 1+ proteinuria, 1+ hematuria, and no ketones, glucose, or urobilinogen. The serum urea nitrogen level is 36 mg/dL and the creatinine level is 32 mg/dL. A renal biopsy specimen shows diffuse mesangial proliferation and electron-dense deposits in the mesangium. Which of the following mechanisms is most likely to produce these findings?
A. Deposition of immune complexes containing IgA
B. Formation of antibodies against type IV collagen
C. Virus-mediated injury to the glomeruli
D. Cytokine-mediated injury to the capillaries
E. Congenital defects in the structure of glomerular basement membranes
【参考答案】 A

A 3-year-old girl has complained of abdominal pain for the past month. On physical examination, she is febrile, and palpation of the abdomen shows a tender mass on the right. Laboratory studies show hematuria without proteinuria. An abdominal CT scan shows a 12-cm circumscribed, solid mass in the right kidney. A right nephrectomy is performed, and the gross appearance of the mass is shown above. Which of the following is the most likely diagnosis?
A. Angiomyolipoma
B. Interstitial cell tumor
C. Renal cell carcinoma
D. Wilms tumor
E. Transitional cell carcinoma
【参考答案】 D

E. 子宫内膜腺体背靠背、细胞异型性显著、核分裂多见
【参考答案】 E

A. 绝经后子宫出血
B. 阴道接触出血
C. 炎症性疾病引起的子宫出血

E. 子宫肿瘤引起的子宫出血

D. 增生的腺体密集排列,伴腺上皮异型性

【参考答案】 C

A. 腺体和间质增生

C. 增生的腺体密集排列
D. 增生的腺体密集排列,伴腺上皮异型性
E. 腺体密集伴腺上皮异型性显著,间质浸润
【参考答案】 D

D. 子宫内膜间质肿瘤

A. 限于一组淋巴结

C. 混合细胞型

B. 中性粒细胞碱性磷酸酶缺如或活性降低
C. 外周血白细胞数目增高
D. 出现幼稚粒细胞
E. 贫血
【参考答案】 B

不符合类白血病反应的描述是
A. 病因消除后血象可恢复正常
B. 常无明显贫血
C. 粒细胞出现毒性颗粒和空泡
D. 中性粒细胞碱性磷酸酶活性增高
E. 出现 Ph1 染色体
【参考答案】 E

A. 肝小叶
B. 中央静脉周围
C. 包膜下
D. 汇管区
E. 以上都不是
【参考答案】 A

A. 肝小叶
B. 中央静脉周围
C. 包膜下
D. 汇管区及其周围
E. 以上都不是

C. 脾包膜
D. 白髓
E. 髓索
【参考答案】 D

B. 开始化疗效果好

E. 慢性粒细胞白血病发病时症状明显
【参考答案】 E

不符合结节性甲状腺肿的描述是
A. 结节具有完整的包膜
B. 结节不压迫周围甲状腺组织
C. 常有纤维组织增生
D. 常有出血、坏死
E. 一般不伴有甲状腺功能异常
【参考答案】 A

不符合地方性甲状腺肿的描述是
A. 多见于内陆山区、半山区
B. 女性患者多见
C. 可引起克汀病
D. 与缺碘有关
E. 与甲状腺素合成障碍有关
【参考答案】 E

青春期甲状腺肿发生的原因是
A. 缺碘
B. 甲状腺素合成障碍
C. 机体对碘和甲状腺素的需求增加
D. 药物影响酪氨酸的转化
E. 碘在肠道吸收障碍
【参考答案】 C

弥漫性胶样甲状腺肿的肉眼特点是
A. 甲状腺弥漫肿大、光滑、质软
B. 甲状腺弥漫肿大、多结节状
C. 甲状腺轻度肿大、质硬
D. 甲状腺内单一结节形成、质软、边界清楚
E. 甲状腺内单一结节形成、质硬、边界不清
【参考答案】 A

结节性甲状腺肿的肉眼特点是
A. 甲状腺弥漫肿大、光滑、质软
B. 甲状腺弥漫肿大、多结节状
C. 甲状腺轻度肿大、质硬
D. 甲状腺内单一结节形成、质软、边界清楚
E. 甲状腺内单一结节形成、质硬、边界不清
【参考答案】 B

弥漫性胶样甲状腺肿的镜下特点是
A. 滤泡显著扩大,类胶质潴留
B. 滤泡上皮增生与复旧混杂
C. 滤泡上皮增生呈柱状
D. 多级乳头形成
E. 肉芽肿形成
【参考答案】 A

结节性甲状腺肿的镜下特点是
A. 滤泡显著扩大,类胶质潴留
B. 滤泡上皮增生与复旧混杂,多数结节形成
C. 滤泡上皮增生呈柱状
D. 多级乳头形成
E. 肉芽肿形成
【参考答案】 B

最常见的甲状腺功能亢进的原因是
A. Graves 病
B. 毒性结节性甲状腺肿
C. 毒性甲状腺腺瘤
D. 甲状腺素摄入过多
E. 垂体腺瘤(分泌TSH)
【参考答案】 A

不符合 Graves 病的描述是
A. 又称 Basedow 病
B. 又称毒性弥漫性甲状腺肿
C. 是一种自身免疫性疾病
D. T3、T4 分泌过高
E. 继发于甲状腺炎
【参考答案】 E

Graves 病中,引起甲状腺素分泌过高的抗体是
A. 甲状腺生长免疫球蛋白
B. 甲状腺生长抑制免疫球蛋白
C. 甲状腺刺激免疫球蛋白和 TSH 结合抑制免疫球蛋白
D. 抗甲状腺线粒体抗体
E. 抗核抗体
【参考答案】 C

Graves 病中,引起甲状腺腺上皮增生的抗体是
A. 甲状腺生长免疫球蛋白和甲状腺生长抑制免疫球蛋白
B. TSH 结合抑制免疫球蛋白
C. 甲状腺刺激免疫球蛋白
D. 抗甲状腺线粒体抗体
E. 抗核抗体
【参考答案】 A

不符合 Graves 病的临床表现的是
A. 男性多见
B. 甲状腺肿大
C. 代谢率增高
D. 心悸、多食、多汗、消瘦、易激动
E. 可伴有眼球突出
【参考答案】 A

Graves 病的甲状腺肉眼特点是
A. 甲状腺弥漫肿大、光滑、质软,伴有血管杂音
B. 甲状腺弥漫肿大、多结节状
C. 甲状腺轻度肿大、质硬
D. 甲状腺内单一结节形成、质软、边界清楚
E. 甲状腺内单一结节形成、质硬、边界不清
【参考答案】 A

Graves 病甲状腺的镜下特点是
A. 滤泡显著扩大,类胶质潴留
B. 滤泡上皮增生与复旧混杂,多数结节形成
C. 滤泡上皮增生呈柱状,伴乳头状突起,类胶质少
D. 多级乳头形成
E. 肉芽肿形成

B. 球后淤血

D. 球后脂肪纤维组织增生
E. 球后淋巴水肿

不符合地方性克汀病的描述是

B. 患者身材矮小,智力低下
C. 与近亲婚姻有关

E. 表情痴呆

C. 声音高亢
D. 多食
E. 易激动

B. 动脉粥样硬化发病年龄早,进展快
C. 引起动脉中层钙化
D. 累及大、中动脉
E. 引起心、脑血管病
【参考答案】 B

C. 细动脉硬化
D. 脂性肾病

His-tologic sections from this mass reveal asingle nodule composed of follicles sim-ilar to normal thyroid tissue. The noduleis surrounded by a complete fibrous cap-sule that compresses adjacent normal thy-roid tissue. Focal invasion into the cap-sule is found. B. Cretinism
C. Toxic multinodular goiter
D. Toxic adenoma
E. Struma ovarii

She reports that she has not menstruated since the delivery. She also says that lately she has been tired and has been "feeling cold" all of the time. Laboratory workup reveals a deficiency of ACTH and other anterior pituitary hormones. Which of the following is the most likely cause of this patient's signs and symptoms?
A. Craniopharyngioma
B. Cushings disease
C. Empty sella syndrome
D. Nonsecretory chromophobe adenoma
E. Sheehan's syndrome
【参考答案】 E

He states that recently he has had to change his shoe size from 9 to 10, and he also thinks that his hands and jaw are now slightly larger. C. Cretinism
D. Diabetes
E. Gigantism

B. Germinoma of the pineal gland
C. Islet cell adenoma of the pancreas
D. Medullary carcinoma of the thyroid gland
E. Prolactinoma of the pituitary gland

## 神经系统疾病

A. 神经元萎缩

E. 真菌感染

E. Lewy body
【参考答案】 E

Which of the following pathological changes is associated with Parkinson disease:
A. Hirano body
B. Russell body
C. Aschoff body
D. Mallory body
E. Lewy body
【参考答案】 E

Which of the following is the major component of neurofibrillary tangles:
A. ẞ-amyloid (Aẞ)
B. Hyperphosphorylated tau protein
C. Apoprotein E
D. Glial filament acidic protein
E. S-100 protein
【参考答案】 B

Which of the following morphological changes is not associated with Alzheimer disease:
A. Cerebral atrophy
B. Depigmentation of substantia nigra
C. Dilation of the cerebral ventricles
D. Neurofibrillary tangles
E. Senile plaque
【参考答案】 B

The substance deposited in senile plaque is the breakdown product of which of the following proteins:
A. Neurofilaments
B. Microtubule protein
C. Amyloid precursor protein (APP)
D. Glial filament acidic protein
E. S-100 protein
【参考答案】 C

A 79-year-old woman who had a medical history of poorly controlled chronic hypertension died while at home. During the autopsy the pathologist finds a recent intracerebral hemorrhage involving the putamen. Histologic sections from this area reveal hyaline material deposited in the walls of cerebral arterioles, an abnormality that is associated with the formation of which of the following types of aneurysm?
A. Atherosclerotic aneurysm
B. Berry aneurysm
C. Charcot-Bouchard aneurysm formed in chronic hypertension
D. Septic aneurysm
E. Saccular aneurysm
【参考答案】 C

A. Alzheimer disease
B. Cerebrovascular disease
C. Parkinson disease
D. Pick diseasemaldinisong
E. Prion-associated disorders

A. Hirano body
B. Russell body
C. Aschoff body
D. Mallory body
E. Lewy body
【参考答案】 A

B. Microtubule protein

D. Glial filament acidic protein

B. Basal ganglia
C. Substantia nigra
D. Cerebellum
E. Spinal cord
【参考答案】 C

Which of the following is the basic pathological change of Parkinson disease:
A. Loss of dopamine-secreting neurons of the substantia nigra
B. Atrophy of the cerebral temporal lobe
C. Loss of neurons in the frontal lobe
D. Loss of cerebral cortical neurons
E. Loss of anterior horn motor neurons of the spinal cord

## 传染病
结核病的免疫反应属
A. I型超敏反应
B. II 型超敏反应
C. III 型超敏反应
D. IV 型超敏反应
E. 以上都不是
【参考答案】 D

与结核杆菌的毒力有关的是
A. 脂质
B. 多糖
C. 蛋白
D. 外毒素
E. 内毒素
【参考答案】 A

结核结节主要细胞成分是
A. 纤维母细胞
B. 类上皮细胞
C. Langhans 巨细胞
D. 淋巴细胞
E. 中性粒细胞
【参考答案】 B

不符合结核病的描述是
A. 主要由鸟型结核杆菌引起
B. 主要经呼吸道传播
C. 形成结核性肉芽肿
D. 结核性肉芽肿具有诊断意义
E. 干酪样坏死具有特异性
【参考答案】 A

当机体抵抗力低下、结核杆菌毒力强时，结核病变呈
A. 渗出性病变
B. 增生性病变
C. 纤维化
D. 吸收、消散
E. 钙化
【参考答案】 A

人体内吞噬消灭结核杆菌的细胞是
A. T淋巴细胞
B. 浆细胞
C. 巨噬细胞
D. 中性粒细胞
E. 嗜酸粒细胞
【参考答案】 C

结核病的特征性病变是
A. 浆液性炎
B. 纤维素性炎
C. 纤维素样坏死
D. 结核结节和干酪样坏死
E. 空洞形成
【参考答案】 D

当机体抵抗力强、结核杆菌数量少、毒力弱时，结核病变呈
A. 渗出性病变
B. 增生性病变
C. 坏死性病变
D. 病变扩大
E. 蔓延扩散
【参考答案】 B

符合原发性肺结核病的描述是
A. 不易发生血道播散
B. 病变多从肺尖开始
C. 不易发生支气管播散
D. 不易经淋巴道播散
E. 大多数自然痊愈
【参考答案】 E

不符合原发性肺结核病的描述是
A. 多见于儿童
B. 肺内原发灶多位于上叶下部或下叶上部近肺膜处
C. 不易经淋巴道播散
D. 原发综合征是其病变特点
E. 多数患者自然痊愈
【参考答案】 C

不符合慢性粟粒性肺结核病的描述的是
A. 多见于成年人
B. 肺原发综合征多已钙化痊愈
C. 患者多因结核性脑膜炎死亡
D. 干酪样坏死破入颈内静脉，血行播散引起
E. 肺内形成新旧不等病变
【参考答案】 D

不符合急性粟粒性肺结核病的是
A. 可以是全身粟粒性结核病的一部分
B. 两肺充血
C. 两肺密布粟粒大小的结节
D. 结核杆菌来自肺外结核病灶
E. 临床上病情危重
【参考答案】 D

不符合全身性粟粒性结核病的是
A. 可发生类白血病反应
B. 累及全身各器官
C. 结核杆菌来自肺门或纵隔淋巴结
D. 受累器官密布粟粒大小结节
E. 常伴有结核性脑膜炎
【参考答案】 C

原发性肺结核病的肺内原发灶常位于
A. 肺尖
B. 肺上叶下部或肺下叶上部靠近胸膜处
C. 肺门
D. 肺背部
E. 肺下部
【参考答案】 B

符合原发性肺结核病的描述是
A. 多发生于成人
B. 结核性脑膜炎是主要死因
C. 病变不播散
D. 难于痊愈
E. 慢性粟粒性肺结核病常见
【参考答案】 B

不符合原发性肺结核病的描述是
A. 多发生于儿童
B. 结核性脑膜炎是主要死因
C. 多数患者自然痊愈
D. 少数患者病情恶化
E. 慢性粟粒性肺结核病常见
【参考答案】 E

不符合继发性肺结核病变特点的是
A. 病变多从肺尖部开始
B. 常为新旧病变交杂
C. 主要通过淋巴道蔓延
D. 全身粟粒性结核病极少见
E. 以增生性病变为主
【参考答案】 C

不符合慢性纤维空洞型肺结核的描述是
A. 可见一个或多个后壁空洞
B. 空洞壁参差不齐
C. 病变上重下轻
D. 可演变为硬化型肺结核
E. 可引起大咯血及脓气胸
【参考答案】 B

男性，31岁，1年来，低热、疲倦、乏力、夜间盗汗，并有咳嗽。X线检查，可见两肺上叶多个大小不等的后壁空洞。结核菌素试验阳性。不符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 可由浸润性肺结核恶化而来
B. 容易发生血道播散
C. 容易发生气道播散
D. 可引起干酪性肺炎
E. 可引起肠结核
【参考答案】 C

男性，31岁，1年来，低热、疲倦、乏力、夜间盗汗，并有咳嗽。X线检查，可见两肺上叶多个大小不等的后壁空洞。结核菌素试验阳性。不符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 病变上重下轻
B. 可引起硬变性肺结核
C. 可引起大咯血
D. 属开放性肺结核
E. 易引起全身性粟粒性结核病
【参考答案】 E

不符合局灶性肺结核病的描述是
A. 属于活动性肺结核
B. 病变多位于肺尖部
C. 多为增生性病变
D. 可有干酪样坏死
E. 常发生纤维化、钙化
【参考答案】 A

不符合浸润性结核病的描述是
A. 属于无活动性肺结核
B. 常发生干酪样坏死
C. 常伴有全身症状
D. 可引起干酪样肺炎
E. 可经纤维化、钙化痊愈
【参考答案】 A

不符合急性空洞的描述是
A. 可由干酪样肺炎引起
B. 可由浸润型肺结核引起
C. 多为厚壁空洞
D. 可引起气道播散
E. 可引起结核性脓气胸
【参考答案】 C

不符合干酪样肺炎的描述是
A. 病情急骤
B. 多由气道播散引起
C. 常有急性空洞形成
D. 以增生性病变为主
E. 以渗出和坏死病变为主
【参考答案】 D

不是经血源播散引起的肺外器官结核病是
A. 肠结核
B. 肾结核
C. 结核性脑膜炎
D. 骨结核
E. 输卵管结核
【参考答案】 A

男性，8岁，1年来，低热、疲倦、乏力、夜间盗汗，并有咳嗽。X线检查，可见右肺下叶上部近肺膜处见一边界模糊的团状阴影，肺门多数淋巴结肿大。结核菌素试验阳性。不符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 可发生淋巴道播散
B. 可发生血道播散
C. 可发生气道播散
D. 可发生干酪样坏死
E. 多数患者发生急性空洞
【参考答案】 E

女性，40岁，左侧腰部疼痛1年，尿中带血，时有脓尿，尿中可查见结核菌。肾盂造影检查，左肾可见空洞。符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 左肾结核病
B. 可引起膀胱结核
C. 可引起附睾结核
D. 可引起右肾结核
E. 易引起腹膜结核
【参考答案】 E

关于肺外器官结核病的描述，正确的是
A. 肠结核以结肠肝曲为其好发部位
B. 附睾结核由血源播散而来
C. 结核性脑膜炎的脑顶病变最明显
D. 肾结核病变开始于肾盂
E. 脊椎结核多累及第 10 胸椎至第 2腰椎
【参考答案】 E

肠结核形成
A. 环形溃疡
B. 烧瓶状溃疡
C. 与肠的长轴平行的椭圆形溃疡
D. 火山口状溃疡
E. 大小不等、形态不一的浅溃疡
【参考答案】 A

结核性脑膜炎的病变
A. 以脑底部最明显
B. 以脑顶部最明显

D. 以脊髓灰质前角最明显

E. 容易转为慢性细菌性痢疾
【参考答案】 D

男性,7岁,因饮食不洁,1天来发热、腹痛、腹泻、脓血便。大便检查 RBC、WBC 满视野,并有脓球。符合该患者疾病的描述是
A. 直肠粘膜高度充血、水肿、增厚
B. 直肠粘膜大量纤维素及中性粒细胞渗出,构成伪膜
C. 直肠粘膜粘膜多数浅表溃疡形成
D. 直肠粘膜炎症细胞浸润
E. 以上病变都存在
【参考答案】 E

A. 纤维素
B. 坏死组织
C. 中性粒细胞
D. 细菌
E. 真菌

C. 未分化癌
D. 恶性淋巴瘤
E. Kaposi 肉瘤
【参考答案】 E

晚期艾滋病患者淋巴结的病变是
A. 淋巴滤泡增生
B. 副皮质区淋巴细胞增生
C. 窦组织细胞增生
D. 淋巴细胞消失殆尽
E. 淋巴结结构正常
【参考答案】 D

Kaposi 肉瘤是
A. 血管内皮起源的恶性肿瘤
B. 纤维组织起源的恶性肿瘤
C. 横纹肌起源的恶性肿瘤
D. 淋巴细胞起源的恶性肿瘤
E. 平滑肌起源的恶性肿瘤
【参考答案】 A

艾滋病机会性感染的特点是
A. 单一病原体感染
B. 单一器官受累
C. 感染易于控制
D. 多病原、多器官、多系统受累
E. 炎症反应典型
【参考答案】 D

不符合艾滋病机会性感染的特点是
A. 多种病原体感染
B. 多器官受累
C. 感染易于控制
D. 多系统受累
E. 出现复杂的临床表现
【参考答案】 C

不符合艾滋病机会性感染的特点是
A. 单一病原体感染
B. 多器官受累
C. 感染不易控制
D. 多系统受累
E. 出现复杂的临床表现
【参考答案】 A

在梅毒病变中,浸润的炎细胞主要是
A. 中性粒细胞
B. 嗜酸粒细胞
C. 淋巴细胞及浆细胞
D. 肥大细胞
E. 巨噬细胞
【参考答案】 C

第一期梅毒的特征病变是
A. 树胶肿形成
B. 主动脉瘤形成
C. 梅毒疹
D. 外生殖器硬性下疳
E. 脊髓癆
【参考答案】 D

引起口腔鹅口疮的真菌为
A. 放线菌
B. 曲菌
C. 毛霉菌
D. 念珠菌
E. 隐球菌
【参考答案】 D

不符合淋病的描述是
A. 性交传播
B. 常为急性化脓性炎
C. 多累及泌尿生殖道
D. 可引起尿道狭窄
E. 与盆腔炎症性疾病无关
【参考答案】 E

深部真菌病不易发生于
A. 恶性肿瘤患者
B. 长期使用广谱抗生素患者
C. 糖尿病患者
D. 使用免疫抑制药物患者
E. 免疫功能正常者
【参考答案】 E

不符合白色念珠菌感染的描述是
A. 多为内源性感染
B. 常形成白色假膜
C. 病变中可见芽生孢子和假菌丝
D. 多发生在消化道、呼吸道、泌尿生殖道
E. 消化道以直肠病变多见
【参考答案】 E

不符合曲菌病的描述是
A. 多经呼吸道感染
B. 可引起支气管炎
C. 可引起支气管肺炎
D. 大量菌丝聚集形成曲菌团
E. 曲菌丝有隔,呈直角分支
【参考答案】 E

不符合隐球菌病的描述是
A. 主要经呼吸道传播
B. 在肺内引起原发病灶
C. 经血道播散到脑和脑膜
D. 引起隐球菌性脑膜炎
E. 病变中异物巨细胞内可见菌丝
【参考答案】 E

tuberculoma
A. 结核病
B. 结核瘤
C. 结核结节
D. 结核空洞
E. 结核肉芽肿
【参考答案】 B

leprosy
A. 伤寒
B. 痢疾
C. 梅毒
D. 麻风
E. 淋病
【参考答案】 D

gonorrhea
A. 伤寒
B. 痢疾
C. 梅毒
D. 麻风
E. 淋病
【参考答案】 E

syphilis
A. 伤寒
B. 痢疾
C. 梅毒
D. 麻风
E. 淋病
【参考答案】 C

typhoid fever
A. 伤寒
B. 痢疾
C. 梅毒
D. 麻风
E. 淋病
【参考答案】 A

schistosomiasis
A. 血吸虫病
B. 阿米巴病
C. 曲菌病
D. 念珠菌病
E. 隐球菌病
【参考答案】 A

meningitis
A. 脑炎
B. 脑膜炎
C. 脊髓灰质炎
D. 脑疝
E. 脑病
【参考答案】 B

encephalitis
A. 脑炎
B. 脑膜炎
C. 脊髓灰质炎
D. 脑疝
E. 脑病
【参考答案】 A
